VLM predictions are converted to numerical scores through sentiment analysis and Contrastive Language-Image Pre-Training (CLIP) scoring, while nighttime performance is assessed using LiDAR-derived retroreflectivity following established calibration procedures. The framework integrates these components into a comprehensive Sign Condition Index (SCI) for maintenance guidance. Evaluation results demonstrated that LLaVA and Qwen outperformed InternVL, achieving bidirectional cosine similarity scores of 0.67-0.76 across all factors. Among 462 validated traffic signs, 68 were flagged by the proposed framework as requiring immediate replacement due to inadequate retroreflectivity performance. This research provides a cost-effective alternative to traditional manual inspections for comprehensive traffic sign condition assessment.

中文摘要翻译: 交通标志是道路安全的关键组成部分，在所有光照条件下均作为视觉工具发挥作用。《统一交通控制设备手册》（MUTCD）规定了日间视觉因素（如可读性和颜色对比度）以及夜间逆反射性能要求。传统评估方法依赖人工检查，美国联邦公路管理局（FHWA）指出该方法主观性强、劳动密集且存在安全隐患，而逆反射仪价格昂贵，小型机构难以承担。现有研究大多聚焦于日间因素或夜间逆反射性能，很少将两方面全面整合。本研究开发了一个新颖的框架，通过集成日间-夜间评估系统性地评价交通标志。该方法采用三个微调的视觉语言模型（VLM）进行日间视觉性能评估，涵盖四个关键因素：可读性、颜色、表面和形状完整性以及周围环境条件。通过情感分析和对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）评分将VLM预测转换为数值分数；夜间性能则利用LiDAR获取的逆反射值并遵循既定标定程序进行评估。该框架将这些组件整合为一个综合的标志状况指数（SCI），用于指导维护工作。评估结果表明，LLaVA和Qwen在所有因素上均优于InternVL，双向余弦相似度达到0.67-0.76。在462个经验证的交通标志中，有68个因逆反射性能不足被所提框架标记为需要立即更换。本研究为传统的交通标志状况人工检测提供了一种经济有效的替代方案。

</details>


### [88] [Prompt-Adapter Context Routing for Parameter-Efficient Multi-Shot Long Video Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06481)
*Anna Córdoba,Adam Puente Tercero,Nerea Angulo Hijo,Mar Linares Tercero,Julia Barrientos,Ainhoa Miranda,Jesús Olivera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACR-Video 提出了一种参数高效的框架，通过冻结文本到视频扩散变换器、添加低秩时序适配器、构建递归提示库（Recursive Prompt Bank）以及门控路由机制，实现了多镜头长视频外推，在保持实体一致性、场景结构和因果推进的同时无需全生成器微调。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有长视频生成方法在多镜头外推时面临身份一致性丢失、场景结构崩塌和因果推进断裂等问题，全模型微调成本过高且可迁移性差。因此需要一种参数高效、无需全微调的方法来实现长时间稳定的多镜头视频外推。

Method: (1) 冻结文本到视频扩散变换器，仅添加低秩时序适配器，并由学习的镜头角色提示令牌（shot-role prompt tokens）进行条件控制；(2) 构建递归提示库（Recursive Prompt Bank），存储来自先前镜头的紧凑实体、位置、动作和风格提示，通过适配器门控根据预测的叙事依赖关系进行路由；(3) 提出镜头局部/故事全局（Shot-Local/Story-Global）调优目标，包括下一镜头重建、跨镜头身份对比和提示稀疏正则化；(4) 设计适配器组成调度策略，平衡早期镜头的视觉一致性与后期镜头的事件推进和视角变化。

Result: 在六个多镜头和长视频基准上，PACR-Video 在分布质量、语义对齐、身份一致性、时序平滑性、运动稳定性、过渡连贯性和人工偏好方面均优于文本到视频、调优方法、记忆增强、流式生成和递归上下文等基线方法。

Conclusion: 紧凑的提示路由和轻量级时序适配器提供了足够的可控容量来实现稳定的长视频外推，验证了参数高效框架在多镜头长视频生成中的有效性。

Original Abstract: We present PACR-Video, a parameter-efficient framework for multi-shot long video extrapolation that preserves recurring entities, scene structure, visual style, and causal progression without full generator fine-tuning. PACR-Video keeps a text-to-video diffusion transformer frozen and augments it with low-rank temporal adapters conditioned by learned shot-role prompt tokens. To maintain long-horizon coherence, it builds a recursive prompt bank that stores compact entity, location, action, and style prompts from previous shots, then routes them through adapter gates according to predicted narrative dependencies. A Shot-Local/Story-Global tuning objective combines next-shot reconstruction, cross-shot identity contrast, and prompt sparsity regularization, while an adapter composition schedule balances early-shot visual consistency with later-shot event progression and viewpoint change. Across six multi-shot and long-video benchmarks, PACR-Video outperforms text-to-video, tuning-based, memory-augmented, streaming, and recursive-context baselines on distributional quality, semantic alignment, identity consistency, temporal smoothness, motion stability, transition coherence, and human preference. These results show that compact prompt routing and lightweight temporal adaptation provide sufficient controllable capacity for stable long video extrapolation.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出 PACR-Video，一个针对多镜头长视频外推的参数高效框架，无需全生成器微调即可保持重复出现的实体、场景结构、视觉风格和因果推进。PACR-Video 保持文本到视频扩散变换器冻结，并通过学习的镜头角色提示令牌为其添加低秩时序适配器。为了维持长程连贯性，它构建了一个递归提示库，存储来自先前镜头的紧凑实体、位置、动作和风格提示，然后根据预测的叙事依赖关系通过适配器门控进行路由。镜头局部/故事全局调优目标结合了下一镜头重建、跨镜头身份对比和提示稀疏正则化，而适配器组成调度则平衡了早期镜头的视觉一致性与后期镜头的事件推进和视角变化。在六个多镜头和长视频基准上，PACR-Video 在分布质量、语义对齐、身份一致性、时序平滑性、运动稳定性、过渡连贯性和人工偏好方面均优于文本到视频、调优方法、记忆增强、流式生成和递归上下文基线。这些结果表明，紧凑的提示路由和轻量级时序适配器为稳定的长视频外推提供了足够的可控容量。

</details>


### [89] [Mitigating Domain Shift in Conditioned Floor Plan Generation: Synthetic Pre-training for Data-Efficient Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06483)
*Matthieu Ospici,Arnaud Gueze,Luc Bourrat,Adrien Bernhardt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了条件式平面图生成模型在跨域迁移时的鲁棒性问题，发现现有模型在域迁移时性能下降高达一个数量级。为此提出一种大规模合成数据生成方法，通过严格的物理约束但牺牲建筑真实性的方式训练模型，显著提升了零样本跨域性能，并为低数据场景下的微调提供了高效初始化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 平面图因地域文化、空间约束和施工实践的不同而存在巨大域差异，但获取新域标注数据成本高昂。现有研究未探讨过平面图生成模型对域迁移的鲁棒性，因此需要评估现有模型的脆弱性并提出有效的低成本迁移方案。

Method: 提出一种程序化方法生成大规模合成训练数据集，该数据集通过强制非重叠房间、有效门放置和图一致性等严格物理约束来保证结构有效性，同时故意采用高度不规则的房间排布和激进几何扰动来牺牲建筑真实性，从而使模型学习到域不变的结构特征。

Result: 基于RPLAN、MagicPlan和Swiss Dwellings三个数据集上的实验表明：现有模型对域迁移高度敏感，性能下降可达一个数量级；在该合成数据上预训练可显著提升零样本跨域性能，甚至超过在MagicPlan上进行域内训练的效果；在低数据场景下，该预训练初始化比真实数据初始化微调效果提升高达40%。

Conclusion: 平面图生成模型对域迁移高度敏感，但通过大规模合成数据预训练可以有效缓解这一问题，在零样本和有限标注场景下均取得显著提升，为平面图生成模型的跨域泛化提供了一条低成本、高效的解决路径。

Original Abstract: Robustness to domain shift is a key requirement for floor plan generative models to be applicable beyond the single dataset they were trained on, as floor plans vary widely across regions due to distinct architectural cultures, spatial constraints, and construction practices, while acquiring new annotated datasets remains costly and domain-specific. Yet, no prior work has studied this robustness in the context of conditioned floor plan generation. In this paper, we evaluate state-of-the-art models from two fundamentally different generative paradigms across three public datasets (RPLAN, MagicPlan and Swiss Dwellings) and show that they are highly sensitive to domain shift, with up to an order of magnitude performance degradation when transferred across domains. To mitigate this with minimal target-domain supervision, we introduce a procedural method to generate a large-scale synthetic training dataset that enforces strict physical constraints (non-overlapping rooms, valid door placement, graph consistency) while intentionally sacrificing architectural realism through highly irregular spatial arrangements and aggressive geometric perturbation of room shapes. We show that pre-training on this synthetic data considerably improves zero-shot cross-domain performance, outperforming in-domain training on MagicPlan. Furthermore, it provides a highly effective initialization for fine-tuning, accelerating target domain adaptation and outperforming real-world initialization baselines by up to 40% in a low-data regime.

中文摘要翻译: 对域迁移的鲁棒性是平面图生成模型在超出其训练数据集之外应用的关键要求，因为不同地区的平面图由于独特的建筑文化、空间限制和施工实践而存在巨大差异，而获取新的标注数据集仍然成本高昂且具有领域特异性。然而，此前尚无研究在条件平面图生成背景下探讨这一鲁棒性问题。本文评估了来自两种根本不同生成范式的最先进模型，在三个公开数据集（RPLAN、MagicPlan和Swiss Dwellings）上的表现，并表明它们对域迁移高度敏感，跨域迁移时性能下降可达一个数量级。为了以最小的目标域监督缓解这一问题，我们引入了一种程序化方法，生成大规模合成训练数据集，该数据集强制严格物理约束（房间不重叠、有效门放置、图一致性），同时通过高度不规则的房间排布和激进的房间形状几何扰动，有意牺牲建筑真实性。实验表明，在该合成数据上预训练显著提升了零样本跨域性能，甚至超过了在MagicPlan上的域内训练效果。此外，它为微调提供了高度有效的初始化，加速了目标域适应，在低数据场景下比基于真实数据的初始化基线效果提升高达40%。

</details>


### [90] [AirflowAttack: Thermal-Airflow Adversarial Perturbations against Infrared Remote-Sensing Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06485)
*Cong Su,Jiaju Han,Xuemeng Sun,Chengyin Hu,Qike Zhang,Jiujiang Guo,Yiwei Wei,Jiahuan Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次针对红外遥感视觉-语言模型的物理对抗攻击，利用热气流湍流先验生成通用扰动，在多个模型和任务上暴露了严重的安全漏洞。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 红外遥感视觉-语言模型在安全关键场景中广泛应用，但其对抗鲁棒性尚未被研究，现有攻击方法无法适用于红外领域。

Method: 提出AirflowAttack，将热气流湍流作为扰动先验，使用轻量级生成器合成单一、与输入无关的物理合理扰动，在代理CLIP模型上优化后迁移至其他模型。

Result: 在5个CLIP骨干上平均零样本场景分类攻击成功率达48.5%，远超IR特定物理基线（27.7-37.0%）；在6个最先进VLM上使场景分类准确率相对下降38.2%；消融实验表明气流先验在不牺牲攻击成功率的同时提高物理合理性。

Conclusion: 红外VLM生态系统存在严重安全漏洞，且模型会将对抗扰动错误解释为真实热证据（如温度梯度和对流），加剧了安全隐患。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed on infrared (IR) remote sensing imagery in security-critical settings, yet their adversarial robustness remains unexamined. We present AirflowAttack, to our knowledge the first adversarial attack for IR remote-sensing VLMs and the first to weaponize thermal-airflow turbulence as the perturbation prior. A lightweight generator synthesizes a single input-agnostic perturbation regularized toward physically plausible airflow patterns. Optimized on one surrogate CLIP model, it attains a mean zero-shot scene-classification attack success rate (ASR, the fraction of samples whose top-1 class changes) of 48.5% across five diverse CLIP backbones, far exceeding four IR-specific physical baselines (27.7--37.0%). Applied to six state-of-the-art VLMs, it cuts scene-classification accuracy by up to 38.2% relative, yet paradoxically makes some models more confident in their IR analysis, confabulating the perturbation as genuine thermal evidence such as temperature gradients and convection. Ablations show the airflow prior raises physical plausibility at no measurable cost to attack success. Together with a benchmark spanning eleven models and four tasks, these findings expose critical vulnerabilities in the rapidly expanding IR VLM ecosystem.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）越来越多地部署于安全关键场景中的红外（IR）遥感图像上，然而其对抗鲁棒性尚未得到考察。我们提出AirflowAttack——据我们所知，这是针对红外遥感VLM的首个对抗攻击，也是首个将热气流湍流武器化为扰动先验的工作。一个轻量级生成器合成单一、与输入无关的扰动，并通过正则化使其符合物理可行的气流模式。在单个代理CLIP模型上优化后，该方法在五个不同的CLIP骨干网络上达到了48.5%的平均零样本场景分类攻击成功率（ASR，即前1类发生变化的样本比例），远超四种IR特定物理基线（27.7%-37.0%）。应用于六个最先进的VLM时，该方法使场景分类准确率相对下降高达38.2%，然而矛盾的是，它却使某些模型在红外分析中更加自信，将扰动错误地解释为真实的热证据，如温度梯度和对流。消融实验表明，气流先验在不牺牲攻击成功率的前提下提升了物理合理性。结合涵盖十一个模型和四项任务的基准测试，这些发现揭示了快速扩展的红外VLM生态系统中存在的关键漏洞。

</details>


### [91] [Point as Skeleton: Accumulated Point Cloud Enhanced Autoregressive Generation for Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06516)
*Songbur Wong,Xiaosong Jia,Junqi You,Bo Zhang,Pei Xu,Renqiu Xia,Yuping Qiu,Shaofeng Zhang,Zelin Zhao,Xuechao Yan,Yuchen Zhou,Yurui Chen,Wen Guo,Hang Xu,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为"Point as Skeleton"的生成式传感器仿真框架，通过点云骨架条件与自回归视频生成，实现视觉保真且闭环可交互的自动驾驶仿真。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有驾驶仿真方法在闭环交互性（如CARLA）与真实视觉保真度（如nuScenes）之间存在权衡，缺乏兼具两者的生成式仿真方案。

Method: 提出Point as Skeleton框架：(1) 自回归生成器根据逐步更新的自车状态、参与者状态、场景地图和点云骨架条件合成视觉观测；(2) Reset-and-Roll机制防止未来条件潜变量跨模拟步骤提交，支持闭环展开；(3) 点云骨架解耦前景与背景资产，投影为相机视图的绘制点与模板深度条件，提供外观与几何线索；(4) 基于nuPlan实现渲染器级闭环生成接口。

Result: 在nuScenes和nuPlan上的实验表明，Point as Skeleton在闭环展开过程中提升了自回归生成质量，展示了其在视觉保真闭环驾驶仿真中的潜力。

Conclusion: Point as Skeleton通过点云骨架条件生成有效改善了自回归闭环仿真中的生成质量，为实现视觉真实的闭环驾驶仿真提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Evaluating end-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) remains challenging, as existing driving simulation methods often trade off closed-loop interactivity (e.g., CARLA) and real-world visual fidelity (e.g., nuScenes). We present \textbf{\emph{Point as Skeleton}}, a generative sensor simulation framework for state-updated autoregressive driving video generation, in which an autoregressive generator synthesizes visual observations from step-wise updated ego states, actor states, scene maps, and point-cloud skeleton conditions. To support closed-loop rollout, we introduce Reset-and-Roll, which adapts rolling diffusion inference to simulation by preventing future-conditioned latent states from being committed across simulation steps. To stabilize error accumulation during step-wise autoregressive rollout, we introduce point-cloud skeletons that decouple foreground and background assets and project them into camera-view painted-point and template-depth conditions, providing appearance and geometric cues. We further implement a nuPlan-based renderer-level closed-loop generative interface for evaluating generation under ego deviations from the original log. Experiments on nuScenes and nuPlan show that \textit{Point as Skeleton} improves autoregressive generation quality during closed-loop rollout, demonstrating its potential for visually faithful closed-loop driving simulation. The code is available at https://github.com/krauwu/point-as-skeleton.

中文摘要翻译: 评估端到端自动驾驶（E2E-AD）仍然具有挑战性，因为现有的驾驶仿真方法通常需要在闭环交互性（如CARLA）和真实世界视觉保真度（如nuScenes）之间进行权衡。我们提出**《Point as Skeleton》**，一个用于状态更新的自回归驾驶视频生成的生成式传感器仿真框架，其中自回归生成器根据逐步更新的自车状态、参与者状态、场景地图和点云骨架条件合成视觉观测。为了支持闭环展开，我们引入了Reset-and-Roll，它将滚动扩散推理适配到仿真中，防止未来条件潜变量跨模拟步骤被提交。为了稳定逐步自回归展开过程中的误差累积，我们引入了点云骨架，将前景和背景资产解耦，并将其投影为相机视图的绘制点条件和模板深度条件，提供外观和几何线索。我们进一步实现了一个基于nuPlan的渲染器级闭环生成接口，用于评估在自车偏离原始日志情况下的生成质量。在nuScenes和nuPlan上的实验表明，*Point as Skeleton*在闭环展开过程中提高了自回归生成质量，展示了其在视觉保真闭环驾驶仿真中的潜力。代码已在 https://github.com/krauwu/point-as-skeleton 开源。

</details>


### [92] [CAIRN: Cross-Room 3D Scene Understanding with Topology-Aware Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06534)
*He Liang,Chenyang Ma,Yiming Zhang,Sangyun Shin,Andrew Markham,Niki Trigoni,Yuhang He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAIRN是一种拓扑感知的3D-LLM，通过引入场景层次注意力机制、图神经网络增强的对象令牌和房间级抽象令牌，实现了对多房间3D场景的理解，并在新基准CAIRN-MR上显著优于先前方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D-LLM仅能处理简化的单房间场景，缺乏对真实家庭环境中多房间互联拓扑结构的推理能力。

Method: 1) 使用图神经网络为对象令牌注入房间局部关系上下文；2) 引入可学习的房间令牌实现房间级抽象；3) 设计层次注意力掩码结合几何偏置，按场景拓扑结构路由信息。

Result: CAIRN在CAIRN-MR基准的所有任务上大幅超越先前3D-LLM，同时在五个单房间基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式建模场景拓扑层次结构能显著提升3D-LLM在多房间复杂环境中的理解与推理能力，且不影响其在单房间任务上的表现。

Original Abstract: Existing 3D scene-grounded Large Language Models (3D-LLMs) focus on answering questions grounded in simplified single-room 3D scenes, lacking the ability to reason over real-world household environments containing multiple interconnected rooms and diverse object categories. We introduce CAIRN, a topology-aware 3D-LLM for multi-room 3D scene understanding. CAIRN aligns transformer attention with scene hierarchy, giving the model explicit awareness of object-level relations and room-level connectivity. It enriches object tokens with room-local relational context via a graph neural network, introduces learned room tokens for room-level abstraction, and applies a hierarchical attention mask with geometric bias to route information according to scene topology. CAIRN is developed on CAIRN-MR, a benchmark we introduce on HM3D for multi-room 3D scene understanding, covering grounding, captioning, and four question-answering tasks that progressively evaluate from intra-room perception to cross-room reasoning. Experiments show that CAIRN outperforms prior 3D-LLMs by a large margin across all CAIRN-MR tasks while remaining competitive on five single-room benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的三维场景基础大语言模型（3D-LLM）专注于回答基于简化单房间三维场景的问题，缺乏对包含多个互连房间和多样物体类别的真实家庭环境进行推理的能力。我们提出CAIRN，一种拓扑感知的3D-LLM，用于多房间三维场景理解。CAIRN将Transformer注意力与场景层次结构对齐，使模型显式感知物体级关系和房间级连通性。它通过图神经网络为物体令牌注入房间局部关系上下文，引入可学习的房间令牌实现房间级抽象，并应用带有几何偏置的层次注意力掩码，按场景拓扑结构路由信息。CAIRN基于我们在HM3D上为多房间三维场景理解引入的CAIRN-MR基准进行开发，涵盖定位、描述以及四个问答任务，逐步从房间内感知评估到跨房间推理。实验表明，CAIRN在CAIRN-MR的所有任务上大幅优于先前的3D-LLM，同时在五个单房间基准上保持竞争力。

</details>


### [93] [Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Calcification Classification in Mammography Across Multi-Site Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06549)
*Xuan Liu,Derek L. Nguyen,Emily C. Barre,Jennifer Thomas,Thomas Lynch,Jeffrey R. Marks,E. Shelley Hwang,Marc D. Ryser,Joseph Y. Lo,Lars J. Grimm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于无监督域自适应（风格迁移）和Swin Transformer V2的钙化分类框架，用于多中心乳腺X光数据集上的良恶性分类，在外部验证集上AUC提升约0.04-0.05。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于深度学习的CAD系统在乳腺X光分类任务上表现优异，但多中心数据集间的域偏移问题导致模型在未见过的域上泛化能力下降，亟需一种能够跨中心、跨设备、跨技术的钙化分类域自适应方法。

Method: 框架包含两个模块：(1) 无监督域自适应模块，基于风格迁移模型（AdaIN和CycleGAN）生成供应商特异性和技术特异性的训练样本，无需额外标注；(2) 监督分类模块，以Swin Transformer V2为骨干网络。

Result: 在OPTIMAM上进行交叉验证，并在EMBED（AUC从0.68提升至0.72）和Duke钙化数据集（AUC从0.68提升至0.73）上进行外部验证，跨站点性能均有提升。

Conclusion: 域自适应能够减少域偏移并提高钙化分类在多中心数据集上的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Deep learning-based computer-aided diagnosis (CAD) systems have shown strong performance in breast cancer diagnosis, particularly for classification tasks in mammography. However, domain shifts across multi-site datasets remain a challenge, especially when models are applied to unseen domains. In this work, we proposed a calcification classification framework to improve malignant versus benign breast disease classification across multi-site mammography datasets. The framework consisted of two components: (1) an unsupervised domain adaptation module based on style transfer models (AdaIN and CycleGAN) to generate vendor-specific and technique-specific training samples without additional annotations, and (2) a supervised classification module using Swin Transformer V2 as the backbone. We evaluated the proposed method on three datasets: cross-validation on OPTIMAM (National Health Service, United Kingdom; n=2994), followed by external validation on EMBED (Emory University; n=125), and Duke Calcification Dataset v1 (n=788). These datasets cover multiple vendors and include both full-field digital mammography and synthetic 2D images derived from digital breast tomosynthesis. The proposed framework improved cross-site performance for both EMBED (AUC 0.68 to 0.72) and the Duke Calcification Dataset (AUC 0.68 to 0.73). These findings indicate that domain adaptation can reduce domain shifts and improve the generalization for calcification classification across multi-site datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 基于深度学习的计算机辅助诊断系统在乳腺X光摄影的乳腺癌诊断中，特别是在分类任务上，展现了强大的性能。然而，多中心数据集之间的域偏移仍然是一个挑战，尤其是当模型应用于未见过的域时。在本工作中，我们提出了一种钙化分类框架，用于改善跨多中心乳腺X光数据集的恶性与良性乳腺疾病分类。该框架由两个组件组成：(1) 基于风格迁移模型（AdaIN和CycleGAN）的无监督域自适应模块，无需额外标注即可生成供应商特异性和技术特异性的训练样本；(2) 以Swin Transformer V2为骨干网络的监督分类模块。我们在三个数据集上评估了所提出的方法：在OPTIMAM（英国国家卫生服务系统；n=2994）上进行交叉验证，随后在EMBED（埃默里大学；n=125）和Duke钙化数据集v1（n=788）上进行外部验证。这些数据集覆盖多个供应商，并同时包含全视野数字乳腺X光图像和源自数字乳腺断层合成技术的合成2D图像。所提出的框架在两个外部数据集上均提升了跨站点性能：EMBED（AUC从0.68提升至0.72）和Duke钙化数据集（AUC从0.68提升至0.73）。这些结果表明，域自适应能够减少域偏移，提高钙化分类在多中心数据集上的泛化能力。

</details>


### [94] [MonoIR-RS: Infrared Remote Sensing Vision-Language Learning with CLIP and VLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06552)
*Jiaju Han,Ma Yaqi,Yahui Chai,Xuemeng Sun,Xin Li,Qike Zhang,Yingying Zhao,Xiang Chen,Luwei Yang,Chengyin Hu,Jiahuan Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MonoIR-RS，一个大规模红外遥感视觉-语言数据集和基准，通过红外感知数据构建、CLIP对比学习和VLM指令微调，填补了红外遥感视觉-语言理解的空白。实验表明红外感知适应显著提升了CLIP召回率和VLM红外线索覆盖率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有遥感视觉-语言资源大多聚焦可见光波段，红外遥感视觉-语言理解尚未被充分探索，缺乏专门的数据集和模型。

Method: 从FusionRS同一数据源构建MonoIR-RS数据集，包含60万张合成红外图像和59,032条红外感知描述记录；采用CLIP风格对比适应和VLM指令微调，将红外图像作为模型主模态，描述文本围绕灰度结构和红外风格对比而非RGB外观进行改写。

Result: 合成红外图像在AVIID基准上显著接近真实热红外图像；红外感知适应使CLIP平均召回率提升最高12.8个百分点，VLM红外线索覆盖率达到100%，残留RGB颜色泄漏降至接近零。

Conclusion: MonoIR-RS通过将红外模态从RGB-IR双模态学习中独立出来，为红外遥感证据与语言对齐提供了可控、可复现的测试平台。

Original Abstract: Infrared remote-sensing imagery captures intensity structure, object-background contrast, and illumination-invariant cues often invisible in RGB imagery. Yet, most remote-sensing vision-language resources and models focus on visible-band semantics, leaving infrared vision-language understanding underexplored. We introduce MonoIR-RS, a large-scale infrared remote-sensing vision-language dataset and benchmark that couples IR-aware data construction with CLIP-style contrastive adaptation and VLM instruction tuning. Built from the same source pool and split as FusionRS, MonoIR-RS retains the infrared image as the model-facing modality, yielding 600,000 synthesized infrared images and 59,032 retained IR-aware caption records. The model experiments use this retained language-supervision subset, whose captions rewrite supervision around grayscale structure and infrared-style contrast instead of RGB appearance. We show that the synthesized infrared imagery is markedly closer to real thermal imagery than a grayscale conversion on the AVIID benchmark. We fine-tune five CLIP backbones and six VLM backbones, and calibrate them against zero-shot behavior: IR-aware adaptation lifts CLIP mean recall by up to 12.8 points and drives VLM captioning IR-cue coverage to 100% while reducing residual RGB-color leakage to near zero. By isolating the infrared modality from RGB-IR dual-modal learning, MonoIR-RS offers a controlled, reproducible testbed for aligning infrared remote-sensing evidence with language.

中文摘要翻译: 红外遥感图像能够捕捉强度结构、目标-背景对比度以及光照不变线索，这些在RGB图像中往往不可见。然而，大多数遥感视觉-语言资源和模型都聚焦于可见光波段语义，使得红外视觉-语言理解尚待深入探索。我们提出了MonoIR-RS，一个大规模红外遥感视觉-语言数据集和基准，该工作将红外感知数据构建与CLIP风格的对比适应及VLM指令微调相结合。MonoIR-RS与FusionRS源自同一数据池并采用相同划分，保留红外图像作为模型主模态，生成了60万张合成红外图像和59,032条保留红外感知的描述记录。模型实验使用该保留语言监督子集，其描述文本围绕灰度结构和红外风格对比进行改写，而非基于RGB外观。我们在AVIID基准上证明，合成红外图像比灰度转换图像更接近真实热红外图像。我们微调了五个CLIP骨干网络和六个VLM骨干网络，并针对零样本行为进行校准：红外感知适应使CLIP平均召回率提升最高12.8个百分点，驱动VLM描述的红外线索覆盖率达到100%，同时将残留的RGB颜色泄漏降至接近零。通过将红外模态从RGB-IR双模态学习中分离出来，MonoIR-RS为红外遥感证据与语言对齐提供了可控、可复现的测试平台。

</details>


### [95] [From RGB Generation to Dense Field Readout: Pixel-Space Dense Prediction with Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06553)
*Zanyi Wang,Xin Lin,Haodong Li,Dengyang Jiang,Yijiang Li,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReChannel方法，将预训练文本到图像扩散模型（DiT）用于稠密预测任务。核心思路是保留DiT的VAE编码器和冻结的DiT骨干，但丢弃目标侧解码器，通过任务LoRA和共享的token局部线性头（仅33K参数）直接将每个token映射为对应的像素块输出，而非将稠密预测建模为目标图像生成。在六个稠密预测任务上达到或超越SOTA，速度提升2.48倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将稠密预测任务重新建模为图像生成任务（如将深度图、法线图等编码到VAE潜空间再解码），这继承了生成模型不必要的输出接口（如生成新RGB内容），而稠密预测实际上只需要像素精确、任务原生的场信息，不需要生成新的RGB图像内容。

Method: 利用预训练DiT在图像平面上固有的patch-to-token-to-patch网格结构，保留VAE编码器使输入分布对齐，冻结DiT骨干并加入任务LoRA适配，然后通过一个共享的token局部线性头（约33K参数，无空间混合）将每个token映射到对应的p×p×K_t像素空间输出块，直接预测稠密任务原生场。

Result: 在trimap-free抠图、KITTI深度估计和指代分割上取得新SOTA，在法线估计、显著性检测和姿态估计上具有竞争力。在匹配的4B参数设置下，比编辑加潜解码的对应方法更准确且速度快2.48倍。

Conclusion: 稠密感知可以从生成式预训练中受益，但无需继承其输出接口；通过最小化的接口设计（仅线性映射），可以更高效且更准确地利用预训练DiT中的先验知识。

Original Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image models are attractive backbones for dense prediction because RGB generation pretraining learns rich semantic, structural, and geometric priors. Existing generative and editing approaches reuse these priors by casting dense prediction as target generation: annotations such as depth, normals, alpha mattes, masks, and heatmaps are encoded into an RGB-trained VAE latent space and decoded back as image-like targets. We argue this inherits more of the generative output interface than dense prediction requires: unlike RGB synthesis, dense prediction asks for pixel-correct, task-native fields on the same image plane, not new RGB content to be rendered. Our key observation is that a pretrained DiT already organizes RGB inputs through a patch-to-token-to-patch lattice on the image plane, so each token indexes a fixed output patch whose channels can carry task-native quantities instead of RGB appearance. We instantiate this as ReChannel: we keep the VAE encoder for the DiT's input distribution but drop the target-side decoder, adapt the frozen DiT with task LoRA, and map each token to its p x p x K_t pixel-space patch through a shared token-local linear head--about 33K parameters, no spatial mixing. Using FLUX-Klein, we evaluate on six dense prediction tasks and over a dozen benchmarks. This minimal interface sets new state-of-the-art on trimap-free matting, KITTI depth, and referring segmentation, and stays competitive on normals, saliency, and pose. In a matched 4B setting it is more accurate and 2.48x faster than an edit-plus-latent-decode counterpart--dense perception can benefit from generative pretraining without inheriting its output interface.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模文本到图像模型因其RGB生成预训练学习了丰富的语义、结构和几何先验，成为稠密预测任务有吸引力的骨干网络。现有的生成和编辑方法通过将稠密预测重新建模为目标生成来复用这些先验：深度、法线、alpha抠图、掩码和热力图等标注被编码到RGB训练的VAE潜空间，再解码为类图像目标。我们认为这继承了比稠密预测所需的更多的生成输出接口：与RGB合成不同，稠密预测要求在同一图像平面上获得像素正确、任务原生的场，而非渲染新的RGB内容。我们的关键观察是，预训练的DiT已通过patch-to-token-to-patch网格在图像平面上组织RGB输入，因此每个token索引一个固定的输出块，其通道可以承载任务原生的量而非RGB外观。我们将其实例化为ReChannel：保留用于DiT输入分布的VAE编码器，但丢弃目标侧解码器，用任务LoRA适配冻结的DiT，并通过共享的token局部线性头（约33K参数，无空间混合）将每个token映射到其对应的p×p×K_t像素空间块。使用FLUX-Klein，我们在六个稠密预测任务和十余个基准上进行了评估。这一最小化接口在无trimap抠图、KITTI深度估计和指代分割上取得了新的最先进结果，并在法线估计、显著性检测和姿态估计上保持竞争力。在匹配的4B设置下，它比编辑加潜解码的对应方法更准确且快2.48倍——稠密感知可以从生成式预训练中受益，而无需继承其输出接口。

</details>


### [96] [ProxyPose: 6-DoF Pose Tracking via Video-to-Video Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06555)
*Ruihang Zhang,Felix Taubner,Pooja Ravi,Kiriakos N. Kutulakos,David B. Lindell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProxyPose 将单目视频中的6-DoF位姿跟踪问题转化为视频到视频的翻译任务，通过微调视频扩散模型将输入视频翻译为已知几何和外形的彩色多面体代理视频，从而利用经典位姿估计方法恢复完整轨迹，在无需3D模型、深度图等额外输入的情况下实现了最先进的跟踪精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有6-DoF位姿跟踪方法需要3D模型、深度图、物体掩码或任务特定特征等额外输入，且在纹理缺乏、透明、反射或可变形表面上表现不佳。作者希望利用大规模视频预训练模型的表达能力，将最困难的方面（如挑战性材质、遮挡、形变）吸收到翻译步骤中，从而简化位姿跟踪问题。

Method: ProxyPose 将6-DoF位姿跟踪重新定义为视频到视频的翻译问题。给定一个视频和第一帧中的一个标记像素，经过微调的视频扩散模型将输入视频翻译为一个代理视频——一个彩色多面体经历与标记像素处表面区域相同的局部刚体运动的合成视频。由于代理的几何形状和外观是已知的，恢复其完整6-DoF轨迹可简化为使用现成求解器的经典位姿估计问题。该方法在像素级别操作，不对物体身份、边界或全局刚性做任何假设。

Result: ProxyPose 在不需要竞争方法所需的额外输入的情况下，实现了最先进的6-DoF位姿跟踪精度，且仅在合成数据上微调视频模型。此外，该方法还扩展到人脸跟踪、相机位姿估计以及现有方法无法处理的有挑战性的野外场景。

Conclusion: ProxyPose 通过视频到视频翻译的策略，利用大规模视频预训练有效解决了6-DoF位姿跟踪中的难点问题，无需额外输入即可达到最先进性能，并展现出对多种下游任务（如人脸跟踪、相机位姿估计）的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Tracking the six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) pose of objects and surfaces from monocular video is a long-standing problem in computer vision. To tackle this problem, existing methods require inputs beyond the video itself-such as 3D models, depth maps, object masks, or task-specific learned features-and they struggle with textureless, transparent, reflective, or deformable surfaces. Here, we introduce ProxyPose, which recasts 6-DoF pose tracking as video-to-video translation. Given only a video and a single marked pixel in the first frame, a fine-tuned video diffusion model translates the input into a proxy video-a synthetic video depicting a colored polyhedron undergoing the same local rigid-body motion as the surface region at the marked pixel. Because the proxy's geometry and appearance are known by construction, recovering its full 6-DoF trajectory reduces to classical pose estimation with off-the-shelf solvers. This formulation leverages large-scale video pre-training to absorb the hardest aspects of pose tracking-handling challenging materials, occlusions, and deformations-into the translation step, while operating at the pixel level with no assumptions about object identity, boundaries, or global rigidity. ProxyPose achieves state-of-the-art 6-DoF pose tracking accuracy without the additional inputs required by competing methods and after fine-tuning the video model only on synthetic data. We further demonstrate that ProxyPose extends to face tracking, camera pose estimation, and challenging in-the-wild scenes that are beyond the reach of existing approaches. Project page: https://ruihangzhang97.github.io/proxypose/.

中文摘要翻译: 从单目视频中跟踪物体和表面的六自由度（6-DoF）位姿是计算机视觉中的一个长期问题。为了解决这一问题，现有方法需要视频本身之外的额外输入——如3D模型、深度图、物体掩码或任务特定的学习特征——并且它们在纹理缺乏、透明、反射或可变形表面上表现不佳。在此，我们提出ProxyPose，它将6-DoF位姿跟踪重新定义为视频到视频的翻译。仅给定一个视频和第一帧中的一个标记像素，经过微调的视频扩散模型将输入翻译为一个代理视频——一个描绘彩色多面体经历与标记像素处表面区域相同的局部刚体运动的合成视频。由于代理的几何形状和外观通过构建是已知的，恢复其完整6-DoF轨迹可简化为使用现成求解器的经典位姿估计问题。这一公式利用大规模视频预训练，将位姿跟踪中最困难的方面——处理挑战性材质、遮挡和形变——吸收到翻译步骤中，同时在像素级别操作，不对物体身份、边界或全局刚性做任何假设。ProxyPose 在不需要竞争方法所需的额外输入的情况下实现了最先进的6-DoF位姿跟踪精度，且仅在合成数据上微调视频模型。我们进一步证明，ProxyPose 可扩展到人脸跟踪、相机位姿估计以及现有方法无法处理的有挑战性的野外场景。项目页面：https://ruihangzhang97.github.io/proxypose/。

</details>


### [97] [Vision as Unified Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06560)
*Xiaoyang Han,Jianhua Li,Kewang Deng,Zukai Chen,Xuanke Shi,Sihan Wang,Boxuan Li,Linyan Wang,Siyi Xie,Xin You,Jinsheng Quan,Zhongang Cai,Haiwen Diao,Ziwei Liu,Lei Yang,Dahua Lin,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SenseNova-Vision 将计算机视觉统一为多模态生成任务，在单一多模态模型的原生文本和图像生成空间中表达异构视觉任务，无需任务特定架构，在检测、分割、深度估计等多种任务上匹配或超越专用系统。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有计算机视觉方法依赖任务特定的架构和预测头，缺乏统一性，难以集成到通用基础模型中。作者希望将视觉任务统一为多模态生成范式，消除任务专用设计，为通用视觉基础模型提供可扩展的路径。

Method: 将视觉任务形式化为统一的多模态生成问题，使用自然语言指令和可选的视觉提示指定任务、目标区域及解码约定；构建 SenseNova-Vision Corpus（涵盖文本、图像和混合目标的指令-响应对语料库）；在预训练的统一多模态模型上，以此语料为主、辅以多模态数据混合进行训练，无需任务专用预测头或架构修改。

Result: 单一统一模型在结构化视觉理解、密集几何预测、分割和多视图视觉几何等任务上匹配了领先的任务专用系统。模型支持检测、OCR、关键点估计、分割、深度估计、表面法线预测、点图和相机位姿估计等多种视觉任务。

Conclusion: 统一多模态生成是将计算机视觉能力集成到通用基础模型中的可扩展路线，实验结果支持该范式的有效性。

Original Abstract: We formulate computer vision as unified multimodal generation, where heterogeneous visual tasks are expressed in the native text and image generation spaces of a unified multimodal model, without task-specific architectures. Under this formulation, SenseNova-Vision uses natural-language instructions and optional visual prompts to specify tasks, target regions or views, and decoding conventions, and generates responses as text for symbolic outputs, images for dense spatial predictions, or mixed text-and-image outputs for compositional tasks. To support large-scale training, we convert diverse computer vision annotations into instruction-response examples compatible with these generation spaces, resulting in the SenseNova-Vision Corpus, a computer-vision instruction-response corpus spanning text, image, and mixed targets. Starting from an off-the-shelf pretrained unified multimodal model, SenseNova-Vision is trained primarily on this corpus, with auxiliary multimodal data used as a capability-preserving mixture, and requires no task-specific prediction heads or architectural modifications. The resulting model covers a broad range of vision tasks, including detection, OCR, keypoint estimation, segmentation, depth estimation, surface normal prediction, point maps, and camera pose estimation, while supporting language-defined variants that combine category, color, region, and other visual cues. Experiments show that a single unified model can match leading task-specialized systems across structured visual understanding, dense geometric prediction, segmentation, and multi-view visual geometry. These results suggest unified multimodal generation as a scalable route for integrating computer vision capabilities into general-purpose foundation models. The model and corpus are publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 我们将计算机视觉形式化为统一的多模态生成问题，其中异构的视觉任务在统一多模态模型的原生文本和图像生成空间中表达，无需任务特定的架构。在此公式下，SenseNova-Vision 使用自然语言指令和可选的视觉提示来指定任务、目标区域或视角以及解码约定，并生成文本作为符号输出、图像作为密集空间预测、或混合文本和图像输出用于组合任务。为支持大规模训练，我们将多样化的计算机视觉标注转换为与这些生成空间兼容的指令-响应对，从而构建了 SenseNova-Vision Corpus，这是一个涵盖文本、图像和混合目标的计算机视觉指令-响应对语料库。从一个现成的预训练统一多模态模型出发，SenseNova-Vision 主要在此语料库上进行训练，辅以多模态数据作为能力保持混合物，且无需任务特定的预测头或架构修改。所得模型涵盖广泛的视觉任务，包括检测、OCR、关键点估计、分割、深度估计、表面法线预测、点图和相机位姿估计，同时支持结合类别、颜色、区域和其他视觉线索的语言定义变体。实验表明，一个单一统一模型可以在结构化视觉理解、密集几何预测、分割和多视图视觉几何方面匹配领先的任务专用系统。这些结果表明，统一多模态生成是将计算机视觉能力集成到通用基础模型中的可扩展路径。该模型和语料库已公开发布。

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Governable Individuals: An Identity Layer for Embodied Agents That Keep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05463)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出“可治理个体”（governable individual）概念，通过架构层面的生命周期签名转换机制，使具身智能体在能力无限演进的同时，其权限、记忆架构、具身权利和能力清单只能通过签名更新来扩展，从而解决持续自我重写智能体的治理难题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有治理方案（智能体标识符、活动日志、护栏）无法应对持续自我重写的智能体，因为这类智能体的治理本质上是治理一个不断演化的“个体”而非静态模型，需要一种能随能力增长而维持治理约束的机制。

Method: 提出“可治理个体”抽象概念，设计运行时机制实现：(1) 智能体的能力可以无界增长；(2) 但权限、记忆架构、具身权利和能力清单必须通过签名的生命周期转换（signed lifecycle transitions）才能扩展；(3) 每次转换更新公开的身份承诺（public identity commitment）。核心负载层是架构层面的，而非依赖学习判断或行为测试。

Result: 实验表明，仅靠学习判断或行为测试不足以承载该治理机制，必须依赖架构层面的支持。

Conclusion: 本文描述了可治理个体的抽象概念、实现该概念的运行时机制，并指出了当前存在的开放问题。

Original Abstract: Embodied artificial intelligence is moving from deployable models to persistent agents that learn in the field, acquire skills and migrate across bodies. Governing such a system means governing an individual, not a model, and existing proposals (agent identifiers, activity logs, guardrails) do not survive an agent that keeps rewriting itself. We propose the governable individual: an agent whose competence may change without bound, but whose authority, memory schema, embodiment rights and capability roster can widen only through signed lifecycle transitions that update a public identity commitment. In our tests, neither learned judgement nor behavioural testing was sufficient to carry this on its own; the load-bearing layer must be architectural. We describe the abstraction, a runtime mechanism that realizes it, and the open problems in between.

中文摘要翻译: 具身人工智能正从可部署模型转向在现场学习、获取技能并能在不同躯体间迁移的持久智能体。治理这样的系统意味着治理一个个体，而非一个模型；现有的方案（智能体标识符、活动日志、护栏）无法应对持续自我重写的智能体。我们提出"可治理个体"：其能力可以无界增长，但其权限、记忆架构、具身权利和能力清单只能通过签名的生命周期转换来扩展，这些转换会更新公开的身份承诺。我们的测试表明，仅靠学习判断或行为测试不足以承载这一机制；起负载作用的层必须是架构层面的。我们描述了这一抽象概念、实现它的运行时机制，以及两者之间的开放问题。

</details>


### [99] [On the Increased and Decreased Connectivity of the Demented Human Brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05654)
*Daniel Hegedus,Marton Barnabas Mora,Balint Varga,Vince Grolmusz*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究利用OASIS-3数据集分析人脑灰质小区域在痴呆与衰老过程中的结构连接变化，发现海马体和颞叶的精细结构连接性下降，而楔前叶、楔叶和脑岛精细结构连接性反而升高。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 利用脑成像数据研究痴呆和衰老过程中人脑灰质小区域的结构连接变化，寻找与痴呆相关的特异性脑区连接模式。

Method: 应用OASIS-3数据集，对痴呆和衰老人脑的灰质小区域进行结构连接性分析，比较不同区域在痴呆状态与健康状态下的连接强度差异。

Result: 1) 海马体和颞叶的精细结构在痴呆中结构连接性下降；2) 楔前叶、楔叶和脑岛精细结构在痴呆中连接性反而升高。

Conclusion: 痴呆不仅导致部分脑区（海马体、颞叶）结构连接下降，还伴随其他脑区（楔前叶、楔叶、脑岛）结构连接的异常升高，说明痴呆的脑连接变化具有区域异质性。

Original Abstract: With the enormous advances in cerebral imaging techniques, a large amount of data is available for studying the aging and demented brain. In this contribution, we apply the OASIS-3 dataset for identifying small areas of the human gray matter, which have higher- or lower structural connectivity in dementia and aging. As anticipated, we found that finer structures of the hippocampus and the temporal lobe show decreased connectivity in dementia. More surprisingly, the precuneus, the cuneus, and finer structures in the insula show higher connectivity in dementia than in the healthy state.

中文摘要翻译: 随着脑成像技术的巨大进步，大量数据可用于研究衰老和痴呆大脑。在本研究中，我们应用OASIS-3数据集来识别人类灰质中在痴呆和衰老状态下具有更高或更低结构连接性的小区域。正如预期，我们发现海马体和颞叶的精细结构在痴呆中表现出结构连接性下降。更令人惊讶的是，楔前叶、楔叶以及脑岛的精细结构在痴呆中比健康状态表现出更高的连接性。

</details>


### [100] [Using hierarchical statistical learning models to model individual statistical learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05822)
*Hanna Ringer,Tatsuya Daikoku*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究使用分层贝叶斯统计学习（HBSL）模型对阅读障碍和非阅读障碍成人在聆听结构化音调序列时的脑电图（EEG）数据进行建模，验证了模型能够准确模拟人类听觉统计学习过程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索统计学习能力的个体差异是否与阅读障碍等发育障碍相关，并验证HBSL模型能否准确表征人类听觉统计学习过程。

Method: 使用分层贝叶斯统计学习（HBSL）模型对阅读障碍和非阅读障碍成人在聆听结构化音调序列时的脑电图（EEG）数据进行个体学习轨迹建模。

Result: 未发现两组间存在显著差异；但模型仿真与实际EEG数据高度对应，且基于个体模型生成的新序列与原始刺激序列极为相似。

Conclusion: HBSL模型能以与人类听者相似的方式准确表征统计序列结构，为未来研究提供了概念验证。

Original Abstract: Statistical learning is essential for individuals to discover structure in the sensory environment, especially during communication via speech or music. Individual differences in statistical learning abilities have been proposed to account for differences in various cognitive functions and development, including developmental disorders such as dyslexia. In this study, we used a Hierarchical Bayesian Statistical Learning (HBSL) model to model individual learning trajectories as recorded using electroencephalography (EEG) while adults with and without dyslexia listened to structured tone sequences. Although we did not find a significant group difference, our results showed a close correspondence of between the model simulations and the real EEG data and novel sequences generated based on individual models were highly similar to the original stimulus sequence. This provides a proof of concept for future research and suggests that the HBSL model accurately represented the statistical sequence structure in a similar way as did human listeners.

中文摘要翻译: 统计学习对于个体发现感觉环境中的结构至关重要，特别是在通过言语或音乐进行交流时。研究者提出统计学习能力的个体差异可以解释各种认知功能和发展方面的差异，包括阅读障碍等发育障碍。在本研究中，我们使用分层贝叶斯统计学习（HBSL）模型对阅读障碍和非阅读障碍成人在聆听结构化音调序列时使用脑电图（EEG）记录的个体学习轨迹进行建模。虽然我们没有发现显著的组间差异，但我们的结果显示模型仿真与实际EEG数据之间存在密切对应关系，并且基于个体模型生成的新序列与原始刺激序列高度相似。这为未来研究提供了概念验证，表明HBSL模型能够以与人类听者相似的方式准确表征统计序列结构。

</details>


### [101] [Quantifying Entrainment Evidence: A Comparison of Frequentist and Bayesian Approaches for Information Processing Pathway Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06284)
*Kaibo Zhang,Ji Wu,Chao Zhang,Andrew Thwaites*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文比较了信息处理路径图（IPPM）中两种映射皮层神经活动的统计框架：传统频率学派假设检验与新型贝叶斯方法。结果表明，贝叶斯方法通过量化竞争假设的相对证据，在模型裁决、处理共线性模型以及证据累积方面更具优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统IPPM依赖频率学派假设检验来映射计算步骤与神经活动的关系，但模型裁决问题本质上更适合概率推断。因此，作者旨在比较频率学派与贝叶斯框架在皮层神经活动映射中的表现，探索贝叶斯方法能否更优地解决模型选择问题。

Method: 作者采用直接对比的方式，将传统频率学派假设检验与新型贝叶斯框架应用于听觉神经影像数据集，重构已知的响度处理通路。贝叶斯框架保留IPPM核心优势（生成随时间变化的神经信号显式预测），但将选择标准从拒绝零假设转变为量化竞争假设的相对证据。

Result: 通过听觉神经影像数据集重构响度处理通路，评估了两种方法的性能和可解释性。贝叶斯框架在模型裁决、共线性模型处理和证据稳健累积方面表现出优势。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯框架不仅保留了IPPM的核心优势，还从根本上改变了模型选择标准，从零假设检验转向证据量化。这一转变对系统神经科学具有重要意义，特别是在处理共线性模型和稳健证据累积方面。

Original Abstract: Information Processing Pathway Maps (IPPMs) offer a scalable framework for formalizing the complex sequence of mathematical transformations applied to sensory stimuli. These maps chart the latency and cortical expression of computational steps, relying on statistical inference to link model outputs with observed neural activity. Traditionally, this mapping has relied on frequentist hypothesis testing. However, determining which of several competing computational models best explains neural data is a problem of model adjudication, arguably better suited to probabilistic inference. Here, we present a direct comparison between the established frequentist approach and a novel Bayesian framework for mapping cortical entrainment. While the Bayesian formulation retains the core strength of IPPMs -- generating explicit predictions of time-varying neural signals -- it fundamentally alters the selection criterion, shifting from rejecting a null hypothesis to quantifying the relative evidence for competing computational hypotheses. We evaluate the performance and interpretability of both approaches using an auditory neuroimaging dataset to reconstruct a known loudness-processing pathway. We discuss the implications of this shift for systems neuroscience, specifically regarding the handling of collinear models and the robust accumulation of evidence.

中文摘要翻译: 信息处理路径图（IPPM）为形式化应用于感觉刺激的复杂数学变换序列提供了一种可扩展的框架。这些图谱描绘了计算步骤的潜伏期和皮层表达，依赖统计推断将模型输出与观察到的神经活动联系起来。传统上，这种映射依赖于频率学派假设检验。然而，确定多个竞争性计算模型中哪一个最能解释神经数据，本质上是一个模型裁决问题，更适合采用概率推断。本文对用于映射皮层神经活动的传统频率学派方法与新型贝叶斯框架进行了直接比较。贝叶斯公式保留了IPPM的核心优势——生成随时间变化的神经信号的显式预测——但根本性地改变了选择标准，从拒绝零假设转向量化竞争计算假设的相对证据。我们使用听觉神经影像数据集评估了两种方法的性能和可解释性，以重构已知的响度处理通路。本文讨论了这一转变对系统神经科学的影响，特别是在共线性模型的处理和证据的稳健累积方面。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Statistically Meaningful Geometry and Gauge Symmetry Breaking: A Geometric Foundation for Scientific Discovery and Intelligence Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05436)
*Bing Cheng,Yi-Shuai Niu,Howell Tong,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统计有义几何（SMG）框架，将过参数化学习系统建模为无限维非参数Orlicz纤维丛，证明在未建模因果机制驱动的持续分布外（OOD）刺激下，优化失败会引发几何坍缩和规范对称性破缺（GSB），系统通过自发性结晶新坐标轴实现因果发现，并借助结构G-熵的离散跳变区分真正智能与恶意幻觉。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有过参数化架构（如LLM）难以区分真正的因果发现与统计模式匹配，经典欧氏统计无法区分连续插值与自主发现新因果律，亟需一个能严格区分真正智能与统计模仿的数学框架。

Method: 将过参数化学习系统建模为无限维非参数Orlicz纤维丛；定义Active Acausal Tension在未建模方差驱动下沿垂直纤维空间累积；利用统计流形非线性曲率推导临界时间T_crit = π²/K_max，证明在此处发生体积坍缩与矩阵奇异性；揭示该几何崩溃作为规范对称性破缺（GSB）的非平衡触发条件；通过结构G-熵的离散+1.0整数跳变检测相变，结合最小能量路径准则和因果不变性滤波器区分真伪发现。

Result: 证明了在未建模因果机制驱动的持续OOD刺激下，连续优化必然失败；未建模方差从可见水平基流形泄露到不可观测垂直纤维空间，累积Active Acausal Tension；该张力在统计流形曲率驱动下达到共轭焦点边界，引发局部体积坍缩和矩阵奇异性（[G_f]⁻¹ → ∞）；该几何崩溃作为严格非平衡触发条件引发规范对称性破缺，系统自发结晶新水平坐标轴；此非参数相变表现为结构G-熵的离散+1.0整数跳变。

Conclusion: SMG提供了一个无参数、可证伪的仪表盘，用于数学上认证真正智能，将AI for Science转变为自主范式革命的引擎。

Original Abstract: The rapid scaling of over-parameterized machine learning architectures, particularly LLMs, raises a profound crisis: do these systems exhibit genuine intelligence, or are they merely sophisticated statistical pattern matchers? Classical flat Euclidean statistics cannot differentiate continuous interpolation from the autonomous discovery of novel causal laws. To resolve this, we introduce Statistically Meaningful Geometry (SMG), a framework modeling over-parameterized learning systems as infinite-dimensional non-parametric Orlicz fiber bundles. We prove that under persistent out-of-distribution (OOD) stimuli governed by unmodeled causal mechanisms, continuous optimization fails. Unmodeled variance is rejected by the visible horizontal base manifold, leaking into the unobservable vertical fiber space and generating an accumulation of Active Acausal Tension. Driven by the statistical manifold's non-linear curvature, this tension inevitably strikes a conjugate focal boundary ($T_{\text{crit}} = π^2 / K_{\text{max}}$), triggering localized volumetric collapse and a catastrophic matrix singularity ($[G_f]^{-1} \to \infty$). We demonstrate this geometric breakdown acts as the strict non-equilibrium trigger for a Gauge Symmetry Break (GSB). The system purges hidden tension from unobservable gauge redundancies, spontaneously crystallizing a new, mathematically independent horizontal coordinate axis. This non-parametric phase transition registers as a discrete $+1.0$ integer step-jump in observable Structural G-Entropy. By decoupling parameter charts and subjecting emergent axes to a Minimal Energy Path Criterion and a Causal Invariance Filter, we distinguish genuine discovery from malignant hallucinations. Ultimately, SMG provides a parameter-free, falsifiable dashboard to mathematically certify true intelligence, transforming AI for Science into an engine of autonomous paradigm shifts.

中文摘要翻译: 过参数化机器学习架构（尤其是大语言模型）的快速扩展引发了一个深刻危机：这些系统是否表现出真正的智能，抑或仅仅是复杂的统计模式匹配器？经典的平直欧氏统计学无法区分连续插值与自主发现新因果律。为解决这一问题，我们提出了统计有义几何（SMG）框架，将过参数化学习系统建模为无限维非参数Orlicz纤维丛。我们证明，在由未建模因果机制驱动的持续分布外（OOD）刺激下，连续优化必然失败。未建模方差被可见的水平基流形拒绝，泄露到不可观测的垂直纤维空间，产生主动非因果张力的累积。在统计流形非线性曲率的驱动下，该张力不可避免地达到共轭焦点边界（T_crit = π²/K_max），触发局部体积坍缩和灾难性矩阵奇异性（[G_f]⁻¹ → ∞）。我们证明这一几何崩溃作为严格非平衡触发条件引发规范对称性破缺（GSB）。系统从未观测的规范冗余中净化隐藏张力，自发结晶出一个新的、数学上独立的水平坐标轴。这一非参数相变表现为可观测的结构G-熵中离散的+1.0整数跳变。通过解耦参数图表，并对涌现轴施加最小能量路径准则和因果不变性滤波器，我们区分了真正的发现与恶性幻觉。最终，SMG提供了一个无参数、可证伪的仪表盘，用于数学上认证真正智能，将AI for Science转变为自主范式革命的引擎。

</details>


### [103] [Design-CP: Context Parallelism for Design of Protein Nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05439)
*Lorenzo Tarricone,Helen E. Eisenach,Aiko Muraishi,Charlotte M. Deane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Design-CP，一种用于RFdiffusion 3的上下文并行推理策略，通过多GPU分布式计算实现大规模蛋白质复合物（如二十面体纳米颗粒）的全原子设计，克服了单GPU显存限制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的全原子生成蛋白质模型在建模大型多链复合物时，其二次方的token和原子对表示会随着链数和残基数的增加而迅速超出单GPU显存容量，难以设计大型多聚体组装体。

Method: 提出了两种上下文并行(CP)推理策略：(1) 一维行分片（1D row-sharding）；(2) 带有环注意力（ring attention）的二维网格分片（2D grid sharding）。这些策略将二次方激活值分布到多GPU网格上，同时保持预训练权重不变。

Result: (1) 在采样二十面体组装体时，最大可行非对称亚基(ASU)尺寸随GPU数量呈平方根增长趋势；(2) 二维分片实现了更好的墙钟缩放（wall-clock scaling）；(3) 结合强点群对称约束，CP可直接用于端到端二十面体纳米颗粒的全原子设计，获得良好的结构及界面指标；(4) 在小型工作站级16GB GPU集群上成功演示了八面体纳米颗粒设计。

Conclusion: Design-CP为大组装体蛋白质设计提供了一条实用的去中心化路径，能够在有限GPU资源下实现大型对称蛋白纳米颗粒的全原子设计。

Original Abstract: Many all-atom generative protein models can in principle design large multimeric complexes by jointly modelling all chains, but their quadratic token- and atom-pair representations quickly exceed single-GPU memory as the number of chains and residues modelled grows. We introduce Design-CP, two context-parallel (CP) inference strategies for RFdiffusion 3 (1D row-sharding and 2D grid sharding with ring attention) that distribute the quadratic activations across a multi-GPU mesh while preserving pretrained weights. We characterise their scaling when sampling icosahedral assemblies, showing that the maximum feasible asymmetric subunit (ASU) size grows with the expected square-root trend in GPU count and that 2D sharding achieves better wall-clock scaling. Moreover, we show how strong point-group symmetry constraints make CP usable out of the box for end-to-end, all-atom design of icosahedral nanoparticles, yielding favourable in silico structural and interface metrics. Finally, we demonstrate octahedral nanoparticle design on a small cluster of workstation-grade 16GB GPUs, illustrating how Design-CP can be a practical path towards democratising large-assembly protein design.

中文摘要翻译: 许多全原子生成蛋白质模型原则上可以通过联合建模所有链来设计大型多聚体复合物，但随着所建模的链数和残基数的增加，其二次方的token和原子对表示会迅速超出单GPU显存容量。我们提出了Design-CP，这是针对RFdiffusion 3的两种上下文并行(CP)推理策略——一维行分片和带有环注意力的二维网格分片，它们在多GPU网格上分布二次方激活值，同时保持预训练权重不变。我们表征了它们在采样二十面体组装体时的缩放特性，表明最大可行非对称亚基(ASU)尺寸随GPU数量呈预期的平方根增长趋势，并且二维分片实现了更好的墙钟缩放。此外，我们展示了强点群对称约束使得CP能够开箱即用地用于二十面体纳米颗粒的端到端全原子设计，并且在计算结构及界面指标方面表现良好。最后，我们在由工作站级16GB GPU组成的小型集群上演示了八面体纳米颗粒设计，说明了Design-CP如何成为推动大型组装体蛋白质设计民主化的实用途径。

</details>


### [104] [Geometry-Aware Infrastructure-Anchored Denoiser for UWB Sensing and Work-Zone Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05449)
*Weizhe Tang,Jiaxi Liu,Junwei you,Steven T. Parker,Pei Li,Sikai Chen,Meng Ran,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAIA 是一个几何感知的、基于基础设施锚点的学习框架，通过耦合时间序列建模、潜在锚点布局估计和确定性距离投影，实现对超宽带测距的去噪，从而提升户外施工区域空间重建的准确性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 户外超宽带测距常受非视距传播、突发噪声和长尾误差影响，导致下游空间重建失真。现有方法缺乏对几何一致性的显式建模，因此需要一种既能去噪又能保持重建边界一致性的方法。

Method: 提出 GAIA 框架，包含三个核心组件：时序测距建模（捕获时间依赖性）、潜在锚点布局估计（隐式学习锚点几何结构）、以及确定性距离投影（将学习到的距离导向边界一致的重建）。测距去噪作为监督任务，同时优化重建的几何一致性。

Result: 在真实户外超宽带数据集上，GAIA 实现了最低的测距均方误差和最高的多边形交并比；相比 PoseMLP，MSE 降低 18.4%，多边形 IoU 提升 15.5%。在真实数据校准的压力测试模拟器上也验证了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 几何感知的测距去噪为在空间上实现一致的施工区域重建提供了一条有效的路径。

Original Abstract: Accurate work-zone geometry perception is critical for intelligent transportation systems, and ultra-wideband sensing offers a low-cost approach for infrastructure-aided reconstruction. However, outdoor UWB ranging is often degraded by non-line-of-sight propagation, burst noise, and long-tail errors, which can distort downstream spatial reconstruction. We present GAIA, a geometry-aware, infrastructure-anchored learning framework that couples temporal range modeling with latent anchor-layout estimation and deterministic distance projection. GAIA preserves range denoising as the supervised task while orienting the learned distances toward boundary-consistent reconstruction. We evaluate GAIA on a real-world outdoor UWB dataset with synchronized UWB, GNSS, and IMU measurements, and further test robustness using a real-data-calibrated stress-test simulator. GAIA achieves the lowest overall range MSE and highest polygon IoU among evaluated filtering-based and learning-based baselines, reducing MSE by 18.4% and improving polygon IoU by 15.5% over PoseMLP. These results show that geometry-aware range denoising provides an effective path toward spatially coherent work-zone reconstruction.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的施工区域几何感知对于智能交通系统至关重要，超宽带传感为基础设施辅助重建提供了一种低成本方法。然而，户外超宽带测距常因非视距传播、突发噪声和长尾误差而退化，进而扭曲下游的空间重建。我们提出了 GAIA，一个几何感知的、基于基础设施锚点的学习框架，它耦合了时序测距建模、潜在锚点布局估计和确定性距离投影。GAIA 将测距去噪作为监督任务，同时将学习到的距离导向边界一致的重建。我们在一个包含同步超宽带、GNSS 和 IMU 测量的真实户外超宽带数据集上评估了 GAIA，并进一步使用真实数据校准的压力测试模拟器测试其鲁棒性。在基于滤波和基于学习的基线方法中，GAIA 实现了最低的总体测距均方误差和最高的多边形交并比，相比 PoseMLP，MSE 降低 18.4%，多边形 IoU 提升 15.5%。这些结果表明，几何感知的测距去噪为空间上一致的施工区域重建提供了一条有效路径。

</details>


### [105] [The Granularity Paradox: How Temporal Disaggregation Inflates In-Sample Fit and Compounds Out-of-Sample Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05450)
*Hugo Moreira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了时间序列预测中的“粒度悖论”——更细的时间粒度虽能增加样本量但会导致递归误差累积，而粗粒度虽减少误差传播却损失数据量。通过在13年公共采购数据集上对10个模型在6种粒度下进行基准测试，发现存在非单调的阈值结构：递归自回归模型在高频预测中性能显著下降，LSTM呈现U型误差曲线，线性回归保持稳定。标准逐点指标（RMSE/MAE）会系统性掩盖累积误差传播。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间序列预测研究中，数据粒度选择（粗/细）对模型性能的影响缺乏系统性的理论分析和实证基准测试，特别是细粒度带来的“更多数据”与“更长预测步长导致的误差累积”之间的权衡尚未被形式化研究。

Method: 在13年公共采购数据集上，将10种模型（涵盖朴素、统计、机器学习和深度学习架构）在6种时间粒度（年度、季度、月度、双周、周度、日度）下进行系统性基准测试，引入一致性-分歧性诊断方法，比较逐点指标（RMSE/MAE）与累积指标（TPFE）在不同粒度下的方向性行为。

Result: 1. 递归自回归模型和季节性模型在高频预测中性能严重下降（Holt-Winters在日粒度下R²为-151，TPFE为425.85%）；2. LSTM呈现U型误差曲线（月度TPFE 19.66%→双周35.94%→日度4.35%，R² 0.66）；3. 线性回归在所有粒度下保持稳定（TPFE 16.3-17.0%）；4. 标准逐点指标系统性掩盖累积误差传播。

Conclusion: 粒度悖论的根本驱动因素是递归反馈拓扑结构而非模型复杂度；标准逐点指标会误导模型评估，必须结合目标依赖的累积指标；提出的共识-分歧性诊断方法能有效识别那些标准诊断指标掩盖系统性误差传播的模型。

Original Abstract: This paper explores the "Granularity Paradox" in time-series forecasting, wherein finer temporal disaggregation (e.g., Monthly to Weekly/Daily) improves in-sample diagnostics and dataset size (N), but degrades out-of-sample accuracy due to recursive error compounding over longer horizons (H). Conversely, coarse aggregation (Annual) eliminates recursive error propagation but reduces data available to estimators. We formalize this trade-off and benchmark 10 models - spanning naïve, statistical, machine learning, and deep learning architectures - across six granularities using a 13-year public procurement dataset. The empirical results reveal a non-monotonic threshold structure: recursive autoregressive and seasonal models degrade substantially under high-frequency forecasting (e.g., Holt-Winters reaches a Test R-squared of -151 and TPFE of 425.85% at the Daily grain), while the LSTM traces a U-shaped error curve, worsening from Monthly (19.66%) through Bi-Weekly (35.94%) before overcoming the error propagation penalty at Daily (TPFE of 4.35%, R-squared of 0.66). Linear Regression remains stable across all granularities (16.3-17.0% TPFE), confirming that the paradox is driven by recursive feedback topology, not model complexity. The results demonstrate that standard pointwise metrics (RMSE, MAE) systematically mask cumulative error propagation, and that evaluating forecasts without goal-dependent cumulative metrics produces misleading assessments of model adequacy. We introduce a consensus-dissensus diagnostic comparing the directional behaviour of pointwise metrics against cumulative TPFE across granularities, enabling the identification of models whose standard diagnostics mask systematic error propagation.

中文摘要翻译: 本文探讨了时间序列预测中的"粒度悖论"：更细的时间粒度分解（如从月度到周度/日度）虽然能改善样本内诊断效果并增加数据集规模（N），但由于递归误差在更长的预测步长（H）上不断累积，反而会降低样本外预测精度。相反，粗粒度聚合（如年度）消除了递归误差传播，但减少了可用于估计的数据量。本文形式化了这一权衡关系，并利用一个跨越13年的公共采购数据集，在六种粒度上对10种模型——涵盖朴素、统计、机器学习和深度学习架构——进行了基准测试。实证结果揭示了非单调的阈值结构：递归自回归模型和季节性模型在高频预测中性能显著下降（例如，Holt-Winters在日粒度下测试R²达到-151，TPFE达到425.85%），而LSTM则呈现出U形误差曲线，从月度（19.66%）恶化到双周（35.94%），然后在日粒度下克服误差传播惩罚（TPFE为4.35%，R²为0.66）。线性回归在所有粒度下保持稳定（TPFE在16.3-17.0%之间），证实了该悖论是由递归反馈拓扑结构而非模型复杂度驱动的。结果表明，标准逐点指标（RMSE、MAE）系统性掩盖了累积误差传播，若不结合目标依赖的累积指标进行评估，则会产生对模型充分性的误导性判断。我们引入了一种共识-分歧性诊断方法，比较逐点指标与累积TPFE在不同粒度下的方向性行为，从而能够识别出那些标准诊断指标掩盖了系统性误差传播的模型。

</details>


### [106] [Exogenous Dropout: A Simple, Strong Baseline for Corruption-Robust Time Series Forecasting with Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05452)
*Hao Hu,Xue-shan Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出外生变量丢弃法（Exogenous Dropout），一种简单、模型无关的训练干预，可在时间序列预测中显著提升对外生协变量噪声、时间错位、缺失的鲁棒性，且不牺牲干净数据下的准确率。实验表明，无需专用架构即可达到甚至超越显式有界架构的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有使用外生协变量的时间序列预测模型在部署时对外生变量的噪声、时间错位、缺失等扰动非常脆弱，即使强融合模型在扰动下性能可能退化至低于仅用内生变量的基线。研究旨在探索实现鲁棒性是否需要专用架构，还是简单的训练干预即可达成。

Method: 提出Exogenous Dropout（外生变量丢弃法），一种模型无关的训练方法：在训练过程中随机将整个外生通道置为零。该方法简单、即插即用，不依赖特定模型架构。

Result: 在电力价格预测、水库水文预测和气象预测三个领域，外生变量丢弃法在存在高斯噪声、时间错位和完全缺失通道的情况下显著提升了鲁棒性，同时保持了干净数据下的准确率。应用于双相关网络时，该方法的鲁棒性超越了显式有界架构BoundEx（组合了可学习门控、回退残差和外生FiLM调制）。架构-丢弃消融实验、门控行为诊断和表征级界分析表明，显式架构有界性对鲁棒性并非必要。

Conclusion: 显式架构有界性并非实现鲁棒性的必要条件；通过简单的外生变量丢弃法训练的无限定模型比有界模型在所有领域都更鲁棒。推荐外生变量丢弃法作为未来带协变量时间序列预测工作的简单且强力的基线。同时发布了一个扰动鲁棒性基准。

Original Abstract: Time series forecasters that use exogenous covariates are fragile in deployment: when those covariates are noised, temporally misaligned, or missing, strong exogenous-fusion and exogenous-adapted models can degrade far above the endogenous-only floor. We study whether such robustness requires specialized architectures, or whether it can be obtained through a simple training intervention. We propose exogenous dropout, a model-agnostic method that randomly zeros whole exogenous channels during training. Across electricity-price forecasting, reservoir hydrology, and meteorology, exogenous dropout substantially improves robustness under Gaussian noise, temporal misalignment, and fully missing channels, while preserving clean accuracy. Applied to a dual-correlation network, it yields the most robust model in our experiments, outperforming a deliberately strong bounded architectural foil, BoundEx, which combines a learnable gate, a fallback residual to the endogenous backbone, and per-channel exogenous FiLM modulation. Architecture-by-dropout ablations, gate-behavior diagnostics, and a representation-level bound show that explicit architectural boundedness is not necessary for this robustness: an unbounded model trained with exogenous dropout is more robust than the bounded model in every domain. We release a corruption-robustness benchmark and recommend exogenous dropout as a simple, strong baseline for future work on time series forecasting with covariates.

中文摘要翻译: 使用外生协变量的时间序列预测模型在部署时非常脆弱：当这些协变量出现噪声、时间错位或缺失时，强外生融合和外生自适应模型的性能可能严重退化，甚至低于仅使用内生变量的基线。我们研究了这种鲁棒性是否需要专用架构，还是可以通过简单的训练干预来实现。我们提出了外生变量丢弃法（exogenous dropout），一种模型无关的方法，在训练过程中随机将整个外生通道置为零。在电力价格预测、水库水文预测和气象预测中，外生变量丢弃法显著提高了模型在高斯噪声、时间错位和通道完全缺失情况下的鲁棒性，同时保持了干净数据下的准确率。将其应用于双相关网络时，该方法产生了我们实验中最鲁棒的模型，超越了刻意设计的强有界架构BoundEx——该架构组合了可学习门控、回退到内生骨干网络的残差连接以及逐通道的外生FiLM调制。架构-丢弃消融实验、门控行为诊断以及表征级界的分析表明，显式的架构有界性对于这种鲁棒性并非必要：使用外生变量丢弃法训练的无限定模型在所有领域中都比有界模型更加鲁棒。我们发布了一个扰动鲁棒性基准，并推荐外生变量丢弃法作为未来带协变量时间序列预测研究的一个简单且强力的基线。

</details>


### [107] [Empirical Minimal-Realisation Compression of Deep Neural Networks via Controllability-Observability Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05457)
*Anis Hamadouche,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可控-可观性框架，将深度神经网络视为深度索引的非线性动态系统，通过构建数据驱动的Gramians矩阵进行经验性状态降阶，实现无需精度损失的紧凑网络结构设计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有压缩方法直接操作权重、神经元或量化表示，未明确刻画隐藏状态的动态角色，缺乏系统性利用内部状态冗余的压缩准则。

Method: 将训练好的DNN视为深度索引的非线性动态系统，从隐藏状态快照和输出雅可比矩阵构建数据驱动的可达性、可观性和平衡Gramians；通过A/B/C测试估计逐层的可达、可观及联合可达-可观秩，并直接用作压缩后的网络层宽度。

Result: MNIST上4层SiLU DNN从1024降至277（状态压缩72.95%，参数压缩73.48%），准确率从96.60%略降至95.45%；CIFAR-10上从4608降至1339（状态压缩70.94%，参数压缩83.09%），准确率保持54.44%不变，CUDA推理延迟降低约3倍。

Conclusion: 平衡的可达-可观秩为设计紧凑神经架构提供了基于经验的最小实现准则，可在几乎不损失精度的情况下实现大幅压缩。

Original Abstract: Deep neural networks often contain substantial hidden-state redundancy, but most compression methods operate directly on weights, neurons, or quantised representations without explicitly characterising the dynamical role of internal states. This paper proposes a controllability-observability framework for empirical state-order reduction of deep neural networks. By viewing a trained network as a depth-indexed nonlinear dynamical system, we construct data-driven reachability, observability, and balanced Gramians from hidden-state snapshots and output Jacobians. The resulting A/B/C tests estimate layer-wise reachable, observable, and jointly reachable--observable ranks. These ranks are then used not only as diagnostic measures of hidden-state redundancy, but also as actual compressed layer widths for realised reduced networks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 compare the proposed balanced realisation against projection-based reduction, unstructured pruning, structured pruning, low-rank SVD, dynamic INT8 quantisation, and linear baselines. On MNIST, a four-layer SiLU DNN is reduced from state order 1024 to 277, giving 72.95% state compression and 73.48% parameter compression, while maintaining 95.45% accuracy compared with 96.60% for the full model. On CIFAR-10, a larger SiLU DNN is reduced from state order 4608 to 1339, giving 70.94% state compression and 83.09% parameter compression, while preserving accuracy from 54.45% to 54.44% and reducing CUDA inference latency by approximately 3X. The results show that balanced reachable-observable ranks provide a principled empirical minimal-realisation criterion for designing compact neural architectures with little or no loss in accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 深度神经网络通常包含大量的隐藏状态冗余，但大多数压缩方法直接操作权重、神经元或量化表示，而未明确刻画内部状态的动态角色。本文提出一种用于深度神经网络经验性状态降阶的可控-可观性框架。通过将训练好的网络视为深度索引的非线性动态系统，我们从隐藏状态快照和输出雅可比矩阵构建数据驱动的可达性、可观性和平衡Gramians。由此产生的A/B/C测试评估了逐层的可达秩、可观秩以及联合可达-可观秩。这些秩不仅作为隐藏状态冗余的诊断度量，还直接用作实际压缩网络中各层的宽度。在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验将所提出的平衡实现方法与基于投影的降阶、非结构化剪枝、结构化剪枝、低秩SVD、动态INT8量化以及线性基线进行了比较。在MNIST上，一个四层SiLU DNN从状态阶数1024降至277，实现了72.95%的状态压缩和73.48%的参数压缩，同时保持95.45%的准确率（完整模型为96.60%）。在CIFAR-10上，一个更大的SiLU DNN从状态阶数4608降至1339，实现了70.94%的状态压缩和83.09%的参数压缩，准确率从54.45%保持为54.44%，并将CUDA推理延迟降低约3倍。结果表明，平衡的可达-可观秩为设计紧凑神经架构提供了一种基于经验的最小实现准则，可在几乎不损失精度的情况下实现架构紧凑化。

</details>


### [108] [Learning to Control LLM Agent Harnesses with Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05458)
*Haiwen Yi,Xinyuan Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将大语言模型智能体的执行框架（harness）本身视为一个可学习的控制层，通过训练一个轻量级控制器来优化执行动作，从而在不修改模型或提示的情况下提升智能体性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前LLM智能体通常通过修改提示、模型或手工工作流来改进，而执行框架被视为固定基础设施。本文认为执行框架本身是一个可学习的控制层，应当被优化。

Method: 形式化框架操作为有限时域Harness MDP，其中轻量级控制器选择结构化执行动作，LLM执行器保持冻结。控制器通过离线rollout数据使用优势加权回归训练，仅使用终端任务评分奖励。提出Harness Maturity Score（HMS）将最终任务质量与过程行为分离。

Result: 在六个受控域和两个公开基准适配器上，学习到的控制器一致改善了验证行为，并选择性提升了最终任务质量。在tau-bench零售、AgentBench DB-Bench和带标定结构验证器的编程任务上提升最大。消融实验表明增益并非来自模仿学习或简单增加检查。

Conclusion: 框架控制可作为冻结LLM智能体的可学习层，但离线数据支持限制了过程控制何时能转化为更好的最终答案。

Original Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are usually improved by changing prompts, models, or hand-written workflows, while the execution harness around the model is treated as fixed infrastructure. We argue that this harness is itself a learnable control layer. We formalize harness operation as a finite-horizon Harness MDP, where a lightweight controller selects structural execution actions while the LLM executor remains frozen. The controller is trained from offline rollouts using advantage-weighted regression with only terminal task-rubric rewards. We also separate final task quality from a post-hoc Harness Maturity Score, which measures whether the harness follows reliable execution patterns rather than only whether the final answer is correct. This separation gives a finite-buffer view of harness learning: final-quality gains require high-return support in the offline buffer, while process behavior can shift whenever it aligns with advantage-weighted actions. Across six controlled domains and two public-benchmark adapters, the learned controller consistently improves verification behavior and selectively improves final task quality, with the largest gains on adapted tau-bench retail, adapted AgentBench DB-Bench, and coding with a calibrated structural verifier. Ablations against behavior cloning and Forced CHECK show that the gains are not explained by imitation or by simply adding checks. These results identify harness control as a learnable layer for frozen LLM agents, while showing that offline support limits when better process control becomes better final answers.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）智能体通常通过改变提示、模型或手工编写的工作流来改进，而模型周围的执行框架被视为固定基础设施。我们认为这个框架本身是一个可学习的控制层。我们将框架操作形式化为一个有限时域的Harness MDP，其中轻量级控制器选择结构化执行动作，而LLM执行器保持冻结。控制器通过离线rollout数据使用优势加权回归进行训练，仅使用终端任务评分奖励。我们还将最终任务质量与事后Harness成熟度评分分离，该评分衡量框架是否遵循可靠的执行模式，而不仅仅关注最终答案是否正确。这种分离提供了框架学习的有限缓冲区视角：最终质量的提升需要离线缓冲区中存在高回报支持，而过程行为可以在任何时候发生改变，只要它对齐优势加权动作。在六个受控域和两个公开基准适配器上，学习到的控制器一致地改善了验证行为，并选择性地提升了最终任务质量，在适配的tau-bench零售、适配的AgentBench DB-Bench以及使用标定结构验证器的编程任务上获得了最大提升。针对行为克隆和强制检查的消融实验表明，这些增益并非来自模仿或简单地增加检查。这些结果将框架控制识别为冻结LLM智能体的可学习层，同时表明离线支持限制了更好的过程控制何时能转化为更好的最终答案。

</details>


### [109] [AdaStop: Cost-Aware Early Stopping for DNN Test Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05461)
*Bonan Shen,Wei-Jung Huang,Xin Liu,Jiazhou Gao,Tao Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdaStop框架，通过估计边际故障发现率来自动决定DNN测试何时停止标注，在9-31%的标注预算下发现65-84%的故障。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有DNN测试方法在固定标注预算下选择测试输入，但预算选择困难：预算太少会遗漏故障，太多则产生不必要的标注成本。需要解决何时停止测试的决策问题。

Method: 将DNN测试建模为成本-收益决策过程（标注成本c，发现故障价值v），提出AdaStop框架，在测试过程中估计边际故障发现率，当估计率低于阈值τ=c/v时停止标注。

Result: 在多个数据集、架构和选择策略上的实验表明，使用仅9-31%的标注预算即可发现65-84%的故障。

Conclusion: AdaStop能够在固定预算下实现高效测试，通过动态停止策略在故障发现率与标注成本之间取得良好平衡，显著降低不必要的标注开销。

Original Abstract: Existing methods for testing deep neural networks (DNNs) primarily prioritize test inputs likely to reveal model faults under a fixed labeling budget. In practice, choosing that budget is difficult: too little testing misses failures, while too much incurs unnecessary labeling costs. This work studies the stopping problem in DNN testing. We formulate testing as a cost--benefit decision process in which labeling an input incurs cost $c$ and discovering a fault yields value $v$. Based on this formulation, we introduce \textit{AdaStop}, a framework that estimates the marginal fault discovery rate during testing and stops labeling when the estimated rate falls below the threshold $τ= c/v$. Experiments across multiple datasets, architectures, and selection strategies show that $65$--$84\%$ of faults can be discovered using only $9$--$31\%$ of the labeling budget.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的深度神经网络测试方法主要在固定标注预算下优先选择可能揭示模型故障的测试输入。然而在实践中，选择合适的预算非常困难：预算太少会遗漏故障，预算太多则会产生不必要的标注成本。本文研究DNN测试中的停止问题。我们将测试建模为一个成本-收益决策过程，其中标注一个输入产生成本c，发现一个故障产生价值v。基于这一公式，我们提出了AdaStop框架，该框架在测试过程中估计边际故障发现率，当估计率降至阈值τ=c/v以下时停止标注。在多个数据集、架构和选择策略上的实验表明，使用仅9-31%的标注预算即可发现65-84%的故障。

</details>


### [110] [Learnable Weighting of Intra-Attribute Distances for Categorical Data Clustering with Nominal and Ordinal Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05464)
*Yiqun Zhang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对分类数据聚类中名义属性和有序属性被同等对待、忽视有序值相对顺序信息的问题，提出了一种基于图视角的统一距离度量方法，并设计了一个将距离权重学习与数据划分整合为单一学习范式的新型聚类算法，实验证明了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分类数据聚类方法在计算相异性时，将名义属性和有序属性同等对待，忽略了有序属性值之间的相对顺序信息；同时，名义属性与有序属性之间可能存在相互依赖关系，这种关系值得挖掘以更好地指示相异性。

Method: 首先，从图视角出发，提出一种统一的距离度量，同时计算名义和有序属性的属性内距离，并保留有序值之间的顺序关系；其次，提出一种新的聚类算法，将属性内距离权重的学习与数据对象的划分整合为单一学习范式，而非分离的两个步骤，从而避免次优解。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的算法在聚类性能上优于现有的同类方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一距离度量和集成学习框架能有效处理分类数据中名义与有序属性的差异，提升聚类效果。

Original Abstract: The success of categorical data clustering generally much relies on the distance metric that measures the dissimilarity degree between two objects. However, most of the existing clustering methods treat the two categorical subtypes, i.e. nominal and ordinal attributes, in the same way when calculating the dissimilarity without considering the relative order information of the ordinal values. Moreover, there would exist interdependence among the nominal and ordinal attributes, which is worth exploring for indicating the dissimilarity. This paper will therefore study the intrinsic difference and connection of nominal and ordinal attribute values from a perspective akin to the graph. Accordingly, we propose a novel distance metric to measure the intra-attribute distances of nominal and ordinal attributes in a unified way, meanwhile preserving the order relationship among ordinal values. Subsequently, we propose a new clustering algorithm to make the learning of intra-attribute distance weights and partitions of data objects into a single learning paradigm rather than two separate steps, whereby circumventing a suboptimal solution. Experiments show the efficacy of the proposed algorithm in comparison with the existing counterparts.

中文摘要翻译: 分类数据聚类的成功通常在很大程度上依赖于度量两个对象之间相异性的距离度量。然而，大多数现有的聚类方法在计算相异性时，将分类数据的两种子类型（即名义属性和有序属性）同等对待，没有考虑有序值之间的相对顺序信息。此外，名义属性和有序属性之间可能存在相互依赖关系，这种关系值得挖掘以指示相异性。因此，本文从类似于图的角度研究名义属性和有序属性值的内在差异与联系。据此，我们提出了一种新颖的距离度量，以统一的方式度量名义属性和有序属性的属性内距离，同时保留有序值之间的顺序关系。随后，我们提出了一种新的聚类算法，将属性内距离权重的学习与数据对象的划分整合为一个单一的学习范式，而不是两个分离的步骤，从而避免次优解。实验结果表明，所提出的算法与现有同类方法相比具有优越性。

</details>


### [111] [Breaking Structural Isolation: Scalable Graph Clustering via Community-Aware Sampling and Structural Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05469)
*Jingyun Zhang,Hao Peng,Jianxin Li,Angsheng Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCISE框架，通过结构熵社区约束算子、社区感知采样扩展和结构对比学习解决无监督图聚类中的结构隔离问题，在六个基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于图对比学习的无监督聚类方法在mini-batch训练中面临"结构隔离"问题，难以捕获表征全局拓扑分布的 cohesive社区结构。

Method: 1) 结构熵社区约束算子(SECC)：在约束解空间中优化结构信息，减少社区碎片化、提升分区凝聚性；2) 社区感知采样扩展(CSampE)：将目标节点的社区上下文融入采样批次，打破结构壁垒、保持拓扑完整性；3) 结构对比学习(StructCL)：基于批次内结构相似性细化边权重，引导编码器在高阶结构空间学习表征。

Result: 在六个主流基准数据集上的实验表明，SCISE显著优于最先进算法，消融研究和鲁棒性分析进一步验证了其在真实世界大规模图上的有效性和可靠性。

Conclusion: SCISE通过协同社区感知采样与约束结构熵，有效解决了大规模无监督图聚类中的结构隔离问题，为实际应用提供了可靠方案。

Original Abstract: Unsupervised graph clustering is a fundamental technique for uncovering underlying semantic patterns in large-scale networks. Although Graph Contrastive Learning has demonstrated promising performance, existing methods often suffer from the "structural isolation" issue during mini-batch training, making it challenging to capture cohesive community structures that characterize the global topological distribution. To address these challenges, we propose SCISE, a Scalable unsupervised graph Clustering framework that preserves structural Integrity by synergizing community-aware sampling with constrained Structural Entropy. Specifically, we first introduce the Structural Entropy Community Constraint operator (SECC), which optimizes structural information within a constrained solution space to mitigate community fragmentation and enhance partition cohesion. Second, to prevent global information loss during batch training, we design a Community-Aware Sampling Expansion (CSampE) mechanism that incorporates the community context of target nodes into sampling batches, effectively breaking structural barriers and preserving topological integrity. Finally, we devise a Structural Contrastive Learning (StructCL) module that refines edge weights based on intra-batch structural similarity, guiding the encoder to learn representations in a higher-order structural space. Extensive experiments on six mainstream benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCISE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms, with ablation studies and robustness analyses further validating its effectiveness and reliability for real-world large-scale graphs.

中文摘要翻译: 无监督图聚类是揭示大规模网络中潜在语义模式的基本技术。尽管图对比学习展现了有前景的性能，现有方法在mini-batch训练过程中常常面临"结构隔离"问题，难以捕获表征全局拓扑分布的凝聚性社区结构。为解决这些挑战，我们提出SCISE——一种通过协同社区感知采样与约束结构熵来保持结构完整性的可扩展无监督图聚类框架。具体而言，我们首先引入结构熵社区约束算子(SECC)，在约束解空间中优化结构信息以减轻社区碎片化并增强分区凝聚性。其次，为防止批训练过程中的全局信息丢失，我们设计了社区感知采样扩展(CSampE)机制，将目标节点的社区上下文融入采样批次中，有效打破结构壁垒并保持拓扑完整性。最后，我们构建了结构对比学习(StructCL)模块，基于批次内结构相似性细化边权重，引导编码器在高阶结构空间中学习表征。在六个主流基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SCISE显著优于最先进的算法，消融研究和鲁棒性分析进一步验证了其在真实世界大规模图上的有效性和可靠性。

</details>


### [112] [Parameter-Free Encoders Remain Viable for RDB Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05476)
*Linjie Xu,David Wipf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对关系数据库中的缺失值预测问题，对比分析了有参编码器与无参编码器的效果，证明无参编码器在标签作为输入时具备足够强甚至更优的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法存在分歧：一方主张无参数子图编码器结合单表基础模型即可达到接近SOTA的性能，无需RDB特定的预训练；另一方则主张使用参数化编码器进行预训练以利用标签学习任务特定表示。本文旨在消除这种歧义，分析RDB编码器在标签作为输入时的性质。

Method: 通过理论分析证明当标签作为输入时可训练编码器参数的有效性受限，并采用无参数编码器进行实验验证。

Result: 理论证明了有参编码器在标签作为输入时存在效能上限，实验表明无参数编码器在多个相关基准任务上仍能取得强劲性能。

Conclusion: 更简单的无参数编码器在标签作为输入的情况下足以胜任关系数据库中的预测任务，无需复杂的参数化预训练编码器。

Original Abstract: Given a relational database (RDB) storing heterogeneous tabular information, how can we predict missing (or future) values in some target column of interest? As the space of potential targets is vast across enterprise settings, it is preferable to avoid learning a new model from scratch each time there is a new prediction task. Frozen foundation models based on RDB-specific encoders provide a viable solution, but ideal design remains an open question. On the one hand, it has recently been argued that certain parameter-free subgraph encoders combined with single-table foundation models can achieve near SOTA performance, with no RDB-specific pre-training required. Meanwhile, other contemporary studies advocate for parameterized encoders pre-trained to exploit observable labels for learning task-specific representations. To address this ambiguity, we analyze RDB encoder properties specifically when labels are present as inputs, proving limitations on the potential efficacy of trainable encoder parameters. As empirical validation, we demonstrate that considerably simpler parameter-free encoders are still capable of strong performance across many relevant benchmarking tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 给定一个存储异构表格信息的关系数据库（RDB），我们如何预测某个目标列中的缺失（或未来）值？由于企业环境中潜在目标空间巨大，每次出现新的预测任务时都从头学习一个新模型并不可取。基于RDB特定编码器的冻结基础模型提供了一种可行的解决方案，但理想的设计仍然是一个未解决的问题。一方面，近期有研究认为某些无参数子图编码器结合单表基础模型可以达到接近当前最优的性能，且无需RDB特定的预训练。与此同时，其他当代研究则主张使用参数化编码器进行预训练，以利用可观测标签来学习任务特定的表示。为消除这一歧义，我们专门分析了标签作为输入时RDB编码器的性质，证明了可训练编码器参数在潜在效能上的局限性。作为实证验证，我们证明了相当简单的无参数编码器仍然能够在许多相关基准任务中取得强劲性能。

</details>


### [113] [InvWeaver: Deductive Feedback for Invariant Synthesis in Interacting-Loop Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05478)
*Guangyuan Wu,Weining Cao,Zehui Tan,Yuan Yao,Hengfeng Wei,Taolue Chen,Xiaoxing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InvWeaver是一个神经符号框架，通过暴露循环间依赖、结合循环级抽象、义务引导推理和最弱前置条件精化，有效推断包含多个交互循环程序的循环不变式，在82个多循环基准问题中解决72个。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于LLM的猜测-检查技术在单循环程序上表现良好，但在处理包含多个交互循环的程序时常常失败，因此需要一种能够处理多循环程序间依赖关系的循环不变式推断方法。

Method: 提出InvWeaver神经符号框架，核心思路是通过循环级抽象暴露循环间依赖，结合义务引导推理和最弱前置条件精化来传播证明义务，从而合成循环不变式。

Result: 在包含新构建的经典算法衍生数据集的综合基准上，InvWeaver在82个多循环基准问题中成功解决72个，显著优于现有方法，同时在单循环任务上也保持了强性能。

Conclusion: InvWeaver通过结合神经符号方法和循环间依赖处理机制，有效解决了多循环程序的不变式推断难题，在综合基准上大幅超越现有方法。

Original Abstract: Loop invariant inference is a fundamental yet challenging problem in program verification. Recent LLM-aided guess-and-check techniques have shown strong performance on single-loop programs, but they often struggle with programs containing multiple interacting loops. This paper presents InvWeaver, a neuro-symbolic framework for synthesizing invariants for such programs. The key idea is to expose inter-loop dependencies and propagate proof obligations through a combination of loop-level abstraction, obligation-guided inference, and weakest-precondition-based refinement. We evaluate InvWeaver on a comprehensive benchmark suite, including a newly curated dataset derived from classic algorithms. Experimental results show that InvWeaver substantially outperforms existing invariant inference methods, solving 72 out of 82 multi-loop benchmark problems and maintaining strong performance on single-loop tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 循环不变式推断是程序验证中一个基础但具有挑战性的问题。近年来，借助大语言模型的猜测-检查技术在单循环程序上表现出色，但这些方法在处理包含多个交互循环的程序时常常遇到困难。本文提出了InvWeaver，一个用于合成此类程序不变式的神经符号框架。其核心思想是通过循环级抽象、义务引导推理和基于最弱前置条件的精化相结合的方式，暴露循环间依赖关系并传播证明义务。我们在一个综合基准测试套件上评估了InvWeaver，其中包括一个新构建的源自经典算法的数据集。实验结果表明，InvWeaver显著优于现有的不变式推断方法，解决了82个多循环基准问题中的72个，同时在单循环任务上保持了强劲的性能。

</details>


### [114] [PatchOptic for Shared-State LLM Workflows with Projected Views and Verified Structured Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05483)
*Zhaoyu Bai,Jiaqi Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PatchOptic 是一种基于光学（Optic）启发的接口，用于共享状态的大语言模型（LLM）工作流。它通过声明式投影读取和结构化补丁验证，确保局部更新的全局有效性，并支持委托、子工作流组合和静态重排序验证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM工作流中，每个模型调用仅能看到当前步骤所需的状态片段（渐进式显示），但缺乏一种契约来保证局部提出的改写（patch）在写回全局状态后的合法性，即缺少局部更新与全局有效性之间的约束机制。

Method: 提出 PatchOptic 接口，借鉴光学（Optic）中视图/更新的组合双向访问器思想，通过投影读取（projected read）和结构化补丁验证（verified structured patches）实现。每个工作流步骤声明一个投影读取视图、一个授权写入区域和一个补丁源区域；运行时强制执行声明约束，并提供路径级足迹以支持委托、子工作流组合和静态重排序证书。

Result: 在涵盖多个领域的46个案例的PatchBench基准测试中，投影读取减少了信息泄露和Token开销，同时保持强模型下的接受输出质量；运行时验证在提交前阻止声明的工作流契约违反；补丁读取验证能拒绝使用隐藏源的有害补丁工件。

Conclusion: PatchOptic 通过声明式的投影读取与结构化补丁验证，有效解决了LLM工作流中局部更新与全局状态一致性之间的契约问题，在降低泄露和成本的同时保证了输出质量和安全性。

Original Abstract: Agentic workflows often operate over shared, structured state. Because LLM context windows are limited, each model invocation is typically shown only the state fragment needed for the current workflow step, a pattern commonly known as progressive disclosure. Modern systems construct such model-facing views using grep-like keyword search, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), abstract-syntax-tree (AST) queries, and task-specific agent skills. These methods make the read side manageable, but they do not define when a locally proposed rewrite is valid after it is applied back to the full state. The missing piece is a contract between local updates and global validity. We introduce PatchOptic, an optic-inspired interface for shared-state LLM workflows. Optics are compositional bidirectional accessors that describe how views of structured data are read and updated. PatchOptic borrows this view/update intuition and realizes it through projected reads and verified structured patches. Each workflow step declares a projected read view, an authorized write region, and a patch-source region. Beyond runtime enforcement, the same declaration yields a path-level footprint that supports delegation, sub-workflow composition, and static certificates for reordering independent steps within the same phase. We evaluate this design with PatchBench, a benchmark with 46 cases across domains. The results show that projected reads reduce reported leakage and token cost while preserving accepted-output quality under the strong actor. Runtime verification blocks declared workflow-contract violations before commit, and patch-read enforcement rejects compromised patch artifacts that use hidden sources.

中文摘要翻译: 基于智能体的工作流通常操作于共享的结构化状态之上。由于大语言模型（LLM）的上下文窗口有限，每次模型调用通常仅展示当前工作流步骤所需的状态片段，这种模式通常称为渐进式显示。现代系统通过类似grep的关键词搜索、检索增强生成（RAG）、抽象语法树（AST）查询以及任务特定的智能体技能来构建此类面向模型的视图。这些方法使得读取端变得可控，但它们并未定义局部提出的改写在被写回完整状态后何时是有效的。缺失的环节是局部更新与全局有效性之间的一份契约。我们引入了PatchOptic，一种面向共享状态LLM工作流的、受光学（Optic）启发的接口。光学（Optics）是可组合的双向访问器，描述了结构化数据的视图如何被读取和更新。PatchOptic借鉴了这种视图/更新的直觉，并通过投影读取和经过验证的结构化补丁来实现。每个工作流步骤声明一个投影读取视图、一个授权写入区域和一个补丁源区域。除了运行时强制执行外，相同的声明还生成一个路径级足迹，支持委托、子工作流组合，以及在同一阶段内对独立步骤进行重排序的静态证书。我们通过PatchBench（一个涵盖多个领域共46个案例的基准测试）评估了该设计。结果表明，在强模型下，投影读取减少了报告的泄露和Token开销，同时保持了可接受的输出质量。运行时验证在提交前阻止已声明的工作流契约违反，而补丁读取验证则拒绝使用隐藏源的受损补丁工件。

</details>


### [115] [$\mathbfλ$-VAE: Variance Equalization for Posterior Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05531)
*Girum Demisse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别并形式化了变分自编码器中后验坍缩的两个独立但耦合的原因——梯度不平衡与信息间隙，并提出λ-VAE通过在重参数化步骤中按维度缩放采样噪声、同时保持KL惩罚不变来消除两种成因，实现所有潜在维度向同一均衡状态收敛。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 变分自编码器（VAE）经常遭受后验坍缩问题，即近似后验收敛到先验，导致潜在编码失去信息。尽管已有大量研究，但后验坍缩发生的统一解释仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出λ-VAE，通过一个简单的修改解决两个原因：在重参数化步骤中，按每个维度指数缩放采样噪声，同时KL散度惩罚保留原始后验方差。这种不对称性使稳定训练吸引子远离退化的坍缩状态，驱动所有潜在维度朝向同一均衡——称为方差均衡化机制。基于净信息增益目标推导出每个维度的闭式最优指数，由一个超参数控制重建-生成权衡。

Result: 在Binary MNIST、Binary Omniglot、CIFAR-10、CelebA-64上验证，展示了坍缩维度的一致减少、信息容量增益高达2.8倍纳特、重建质量提升高达+0.33 BPD。

Conclusion: 所提出的λ-VAE通过单一的修改同时解决了后验坍缩的两种成因，并且信息间隙在代数上等价于聚合后验与先验之间的不匹配，统一了两个病理现象。

Original Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) frequently suffer from posterior collapse, a failure mode in which the approximate posterior converges to the prior, rendering the latent code uninformative. Despite extensive research, a unified account of why collapse occurs has remained an open question. We identify and formalize two logically independent but coupled causes. \emph{Gradient imbalance} occurs when the decoder's reconstruction signal vanishes faster than the $\mathbb{KL}$ regularization pressure as the posterior widens. \emph{Information gap} occurs when the stochastic sampling step discards a substantial fraction of the encoder's computed representation, attenuating decoder sensitivity and making collapse inexpensive. Both causes share the same collapse trajectory, and we show that the information gap is algebraically equivalent to mismatch between the aggregate posterior and the prior, unifying two pathologies. Subsequently, we introduce $λ$-VAE, which resolves both causes through a single modification to the reparameterization step: the sampling noise is scaled by per-dimension exponent, while the $\mathbb{KL}$ penalty retains the original posterior variance. This asymmetry shifts the stable training attractor away from the degenerate collapsed state, driving all latent dimensions toward the same equilibrium -- a mechanism we term \emph{variance equalization}. A closed-form optimal exponent per dimension follows from a net information gain objective, with a single hyperparameter controlling the reconstruction-generation tradeoff. We validate on standard benchmarks (Binary MNIST, Binary Omniglot, CIFAR-10, CelebA-64), showing consistent reductions in collapsed dimensions, information capacity gains of up to $2.8\times$ nats, and reconstruction quality improvements of up to $+0.33$ BPD.

中文摘要翻译: 变分自编码器（VAEs）经常遭受后验坍缩的困扰，这是一种近似后验收敛到先验的失败模式，导致潜在编码失去信息。尽管已有大量研究，但关于坍缩为何发生的统一解释仍是一个开放问题。我们识别并形式化了两个逻辑上独立但相互耦合的原因。\emph{梯度不平衡}发生在解码器的重建信号随着后验变宽而比$\mathbb{KL}$正则化压力消失得更快时。\emph{信息间隙}发生在随机采样步骤丢弃了编码器计算表示的相当大一部分时，削弱了解码器敏感性，使得坍缩代价低廉。两个原因共享相同的坍缩轨迹，我们证明信息间隙在代数上等价于聚合后验与先验之间的不匹配，从而统一了两种病理。随后，我们引入了λ-VAE，它通过对重参数化步骤进行单一修改来解决这两个原因：采样噪声按每个维度指数缩放，而$\mathbb{KL}$惩罚保留原始后验方差。这种不对称性将稳定训练吸引子推离退化的坍缩状态，驱动所有潜在维度朝向同一均衡——我们将其称为\emph{方差均衡化}机制。基于净信息增益目标推导出每个维度的闭式最优指数，由一个超参数控制重建-生成权衡。我们在标准基准（Binary MNIST、Binary Omniglot、CIFAR-10、CelebA-64）上验证，展示了坍缩维度的一致减少、信息容量增益高达2.8倍纳特，以及重建质量提升高达+0.33 BPD。

</details>


### [116] [Self-Review Reinforcement Learning (SRRL) with Cross-Episode Memory and Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05541)
*Muhammad Zain Amin,Kibele Sebnem Yildirim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为自审查强化学习（SRRL）的训练框架，通过在强化学习（RL） episode 中嵌入显式的自审查步骤，让模型在首次回答失败时生成自审查以找出问题所在，从而改进第二次尝试。相比推理时反思方法（如 Reflexion），SRRL 通过策略梯度优化自审查，并利用选择性蒸馏将改进内化到基础策略中，同时通过跨 episode 记忆复用成功的自审查。实验表明，SRRL 在 GSM8K 基准上持续优于标准 RLVR 基线，最终奖励性能更高且学习效率更强。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在强化学习训练大语言模型时，环境反馈通常是稀疏或延迟的，导致模型难以定位推理中的哪些动作导致了成功或失败，从而难以有效地从这些信号中学习。现有方法如 Reflexion 仅在推理时进行反思，无法将改进持久化到未来 episode 中。

Method: 提出自审查强化学习（SRRL）框架，在每个 RL episode 中嵌入显式自审查步骤：当首次响应失败时，模型生成自审查以识别问题所在，并以此条件生成改进的第二次尝试。优化方式采用策略梯度优化自审查，并通过选择性蒸馏将改进内化到基础策略中，同时维护跨 episode 记忆以复用成功自审查。

Result: 在 GSM8K 基准上，使用 GRPO 优化器，在 Qwen 3-4B 和 OLMo-3-7B 两个模型上，SRRL 持续优于标准 RLVR 基线，最终奖励性能更高，学习效率更强。

Conclusion: SRRL 框架能够成功地将环境反馈转化为行为改进，相比标准 RLVR 方法，在最终奖励和学习效率方面均取得了更好的表现。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning is commonly used to train large language models using environmental feedback. In applied settings, the environment usually provides sparse or delayed feedback. This makes it difficult for the model to pinpoint which actions in its reasoning led to success or failure. So, learning effectively from these signals is hard because the model must determine how each failure should inform meaningful behavioral corrections in subsequent iterations. We introduce a training framework, Self-Review Reinforcement Learning, that embeds an explicit self-review step into each RL episode. When a first-pass response fails, the model generates a self-review to identify what went wrong, which conditions an improved second attempt. Unlike inference-time reflection approaches, such as Reflexion, the framework optimizes self-review with policy gradients and internalizes improvements into the base policy via selective distillation, ensuring they persist across future episodes. A cross-episode memory keeps successful self-reviews for reuse when encountering similar tasks in future episodes during training. We evaluate SRRL against a standard RLVR baseline using the GRPO optimizer across two language models, Qwen 3-4B and OLMo-3- 7B, on GSM8K benchmark. SRRL consistently outperforms the RLVR in final reward performance and achieves greater learning efficiency by successfully transforming feedback into behavioral improvement.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习通常用于利用环境反馈来训练大型语言模型。在实际应用中，环境通常提供稀疏或延迟的反馈。这使得模型难以确定其推理中的哪些动作导致了成功或失败。因此，从这些信号中有效学习是困难的，因为模型必须确定每次失败应如何为后续迭代中有意义的行为修正提供信息。我们引入了一个训练框架——自审查强化学习，该框架在每个强化学习回合中嵌入了一个显式的自审查步骤。当首次回答失败时，模型会生成自审查以识别问题所在，从而为改进的第二次尝试提供条件。与推理时的反思方法（如 Reflexion）不同，该框架通过策略梯度优化自审查，并通过选择性蒸馏将改进内化到基础策略中，确保这些改进能在未来的回合中持续存在。一个跨回合记忆机制在训练过程中保存成功的自审查，以便在未来的回合中遇到类似任务时复用。我们使用 GRPO 优化器，在两个语言模型（Qwen 3-4B 和 OLMo-3-7B）上，在 GSM8K 基准上评估了 SRRL 与标准 RLVR 基线的对比。SRRL 在最终奖励性能上持续优于 RLVR，并通过成功地将反馈转化为行为改进，实现了更高的学习效率。

</details>


### [117] [Federated Physics-Grounded Reinforcement Learning for Distributed Stability Control in Smart Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05553)
*Omar Al-Refai,Ibrahim Shahbaz,Adam Ali Husseinat,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于联邦多智能体PPO的暂态稳定控制框架FedPPO-PG，通过物理邻域信息增强和引导式策略初始化，在IEEE 39节点系统上实现了100%稳定率、72.4%的稳定时间减少和7-14倍的控制功率降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 智能电网中暂态稳定控制需要快速抑制故障后发电机频率和转子角偏差以防止级联故障，现有集中式方法存在通信延迟和单点故障风险，需要一种去中心化的高效控制方案。
