This way, CAD and CAE can be tightly integrated in a deep neural network without the time-consuming model/data exchange process. In the data-driven case, SplineNet can be used as the trunk net of Deep Operator Networks (DeepONet) to provide interpretability. Given such a trained network and unseen input data, results can be immediately obtained without retraining the network or repeatedly performing the traditional workflow for analysis. In the end, a variety of numerical examples are studied to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, especially when real-world complex geometries are involved.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种新颖的等几何深度学习方法，称为SplineNet，用于复杂几何壳体结构的无缝设计与分析。该方法基于水密样条表示（例如分析适用的非结构化T样条），并在神经网络中实现了计算机辅助设计（CAD）模型的精确几何描述。采用Bézier提取构建网络架构，其中Bernstein多项式作为非线性激活函数。SplineNet可应用于无数据或数据驱动方式。在无数据情况下，能量公式可以自然地作为损失项引入，满足计算机辅助工程（CAE）的需求并能精确计算。特别地，采用Kirchhoff-Love（KL）模型求解壳体结构的力学行为。这样，CAD和CAE可以在深度神经网络中紧密集成，无需耗时的模型/数据交换过程。在数据驱动情况下，SplineNet可作为深度算子网络（DeepONet）的主干网络提供可解释性。给定这样一个训练好的网络和未见过的输入数据，无需重新训练网络或重复执行传统的分析工作流程即可立即获得结果。最后，通过多种数值示例验证了该方法的有效性，特别是在涉及真实世界复杂几何形状时。

</details>


### [147] [Scalable Perturbation Learning for Online Self-Supervised Echo State Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06079)
*Taiki Yamada,Kantaro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扰动的在线自监督学习规则，通过正交分解将扰动维度从储层大小降低到输入大小，从而在回波状态网络中同时实现了自监督适应、在线学习和标量反馈扰动学习，避免了方差随储层维度增长的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 智能系统需要同时满足自监督适应、在线学习和基于扰动的内存高效实现，但这些要求在高维系统中相互冲突——基于扰动的学习方差会随扰动变量维度增长，导致无法扩展到高维储层网络。

Method: 对自监督学习代价函数进行正交分解，将其分离为输入依赖分量和由固定ESN参数决定的冗余分量；仅对输入依赖分量进行扰动，将有效扰动维度从储层维度降低到输入维度。

Result: 所提方法在保持自监督适应、在线学习和标量反馈扰动学习的同时，避免了随储层大小增长的方差爆炸问题。

Conclusion: 提出了一种设计原则：在线学习应限制在动态必要的低维目标分量上，以实现可扩展且兼容硬件的学习系统。

Original Abstract: Intelligent systems should not only solve tasks but also adapt under real-world constraints. Autonomous adaptation via self-supervised learning, sequential adaptation via online learning, and memory-efficient implementation via perturbation-based learning are important requirements for such systems. However, these requirements are generally in tension for high-dimensional systems, because perturbation-based learning suffers from variance that grows with the dimension of the perturbed variables.
  In this study, we focus on echo state networks (ESNs), where this tension naturally arises in large reservoirs. We propose a perturbation-based learning rule for online self-supervised learning in ESNs. The proposed rule is derived from an orthogonal decomposition of the self-supervised learning cost, which separates an input-dependent component from a redundant component determined by the fixed ESN parameters. By perturbing only the input-dependent component, the effective perturbation dimension is reduced from the reservoir dimension to the input dimension.
  Thus, the proposed method preserves self-supervised adaptation, online learning, and scalar-feedback perturbation learning, while avoiding reservoir-size-dependent variance growth. This suggests a design principle for scalable and hardware-compatible learning: online learning should be restricted to the dynamically necessary low-dimensional component of the objective.

中文摘要翻译: 智能系统不仅应解决任务，还应能在现实约束下自适应。通过自监督学习实现自主适应、通过在线学习实现顺序适应、以及通过基于扰动的学习实现内存高效执行，是此类系统的重要需求。然而，对于高维系统，这些需求通常相互冲突，因为基于扰动的学习的方差会随扰动变量的维度增长而增长。在本研究中，我们聚焦于回波状态网络（ESN），在这种网络的大规模储层中自然会出现上述冲突。我们提出了一种用于ESN在线自监督学习的基于扰动的学习规则。该规则源自对自监督学习代价函数的正交分解，将依赖于输入的分量与由固定ESN参数决定的冗余分量分离开来。通过仅扰动依赖于输入的分量，有效扰动维度从储层维度降低到了输入维度。因此，所提出的方法保留了自监督适应、在线学习和标量反馈扰动学习的能力，同时避免了随储层大小增长的方差问题。这为可扩展且兼容硬件的学习提出了一个设计原则：在线学习应限制在目标函数中动态必要的低维分量上。

</details>


### [148] [Modeling Normal Is All You Need: Joint Latent Clustering for Anomaly Detection in Multimodal Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06094)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息物理系统（CPS）异常检测中正常行为呈现“大规模、隐式、不平衡多模态（MIIM）”结构假设，并设计了一种基于联合学习潜在表示与显式高斯混合聚类的检测方法，在公平评估协议下显著优于现有深度检测器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CPS中的故障过于罕见且不具代表性，无法直接建模故障，因此需对正常行为建模。然而，标准点调整评估会奖励那些从不检测的检测器。CPS正常行为是多个不平衡、弯曲、薄边缘运行模式的并集，而非单一簇，现有方法未能很好处理这一结构。

Method: 提出MIIM假设集（A1-A10），描述CPS正常行为的多模态结构；设计联合学习潜在表示与显式高斯混合模型（GMM）聚类的方法，在潜在空间中计算异常分数（而非全局密度或重构残差）；采用公平评估协议：原始逐点指标（无点调整）、按难度划分（含平凡检测器拆分）、按流行率匹配的F1、仅用训练正常数据校准。

Result: 在WADI、HAI、SKAB三个真实CPS数据集上，所提检测器在所有三个数据集的综合列和困难（相关/动态故障）列均取得最佳结果，困难子集AUROC分别达到HAI 0.831、WADI 0.726、SKAB 0.610。在MIIM假设针对的多模态数据集上优势最大，在近单模态数据集上优势最小。与USAD、TranAD、GDN三种深度检测器相比，这些对比方法在困难子集上完全失效。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献包括：MIIM假设集、按难度分层的公平评估协议、以及仅使用潜在空间分数（丢弃重构分支）的方法，因为灵活的解码器会忠实地重建困难故障，导致重构残差失效。

Original Abstract: Faults on a cyber-physical system (CPS) are too rare and unrepresentative to characterise, or even to select a model on, so detection must instead model normal behaviour; the standard point-adjusted evaluation, however, rewards detectors that never do. CPS normal behaviour is the union of many imbalanced, curved, thin-fringed operating regimes rather than a single blob; we state this structure as ten assumptions (A1-A10), abbreviated Massive, Implicit, Imbalanced Multimodality (MIIM). We model the normal law with a jointly learned latent representation plus explicit Gaussian-mixture mode clustering, scored in the latent rather than by a global density or a reconstruction residual, and evaluate under a deliberately fair protocol: raw point-wise metrics with no point adjustment, a trivial-detector difficulty split, prevalence-matched F1, and train-normal-only calibration. On three real CPS datasets (WADI, HAI, SKAB), the detector wins both the combined column and the difficult correlation/dynamics-fault column on all three, reaching difficult-subset AUROC 0.831 on HAI, 0.726 on WADI, and 0.610 on SKAB. The margin is largest on the two multimodal datasets the MIIM assumptions target and slimmest on the near-unimodal one, tracking multimodality as the thesis predicts, and it holds against three deep detectors (USAD, TranAD, GDN) re-computed with the same raw metrics, all of which collapse on the difficult subset. The methodological contributions are the MIIM assumption set, the difficulty-stratified fair protocol, and a latent-only score that drops reconstruction because a flexible decoder rebuilds the hard faults faithfully.

中文摘要翻译: 信息物理系统（CPS）中的故障过于罕见且缺乏代表性，难以刻画甚至难以选择模型，因此检测必须转而建模正常行为；然而，标准点调整评估方法会奖励那些从不检测的检测器。CPS的正常行为是众多不平衡、弯曲、薄边缘运行模式的并集，而非单一簇；我们将此结构表述为十条假设（A1-A10），简称为大规模、隐式、不平衡多模态（MIIM）。我们通过联合学习潜在表示并结合显式高斯混合模式聚类来对正常分布进行建模，在潜在空间中而非通过全局密度或重构残差进行评分，并在特意设计的公平协议下进行评估：原始逐点指标（无点调整）、含平凡检测器的难度拆分、按流行率匹配的F1、以及仅使用训练正常数据的校准。在三个真实CPS数据集（WADI、HAI、SKAB）上，该检测器在所有三个数据集的综合列和困难（相关/动态故障）列均取得最佳结果，困难子集AUROC分别达到HAI 0.831、WADI 0.726和SKAB 0.610。在MIIM假设所针对的两个多模态数据集上优势最大，在近单模态数据集上优势最小，这与多模态性的论点相一致；并且该优势在采用相同原始指标重新计算的三个深度检测器（USAD、TranAD、GDN）面前依然保持，这些对比方法在困难子集上均完全失效。本文的方法论贡献包括：MIIM假设集、按难度分层的公平协议、以及仅使用潜在空间分数（丢弃重构分支）的方法，因为灵活的解码器会忠实地重建困难故障。

</details>


### [149] [x-Prediction Is All You Need:Training-Free Accelerated Generation via Endpoint Decodability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06114)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出截断跳跃采样（TJS），利用扩散模型中ODE采样路径上的端点可解码性，在不重新训练、蒸馏或改变架构的情况下，通过提前终止ODE并解码$x_0$估计值，实现20-70%的NFE减少且质量几乎不变。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配模型的ODE采样器通常需要数十到数百次神经函数评估（NFE），这对已发布检查点的实际部署构成挑战，因为现有加速方法（如重新训练、蒸馏或轨迹重新设计）会引入额外的设计成本和训练开销。

Method: 形式化定义“端点可解码性”（endpoint decodability）：在标准仿射概率路径下，中间状态$x_t$及其路径速度可推导出干净样本$x_0$的最小MSE估计量$\mathbb{E}[x_0\mid x_t]$。基于此提出截断跳跃采样（TJS）：在早退时间$t^*$停止ODE，直接解码输出$x_0$估计值。

Result: 在SDXL、SD3.5M、Z-Image-Turbo及三个类条件基准上，TJS可将NFE减少20-70%，同时保持与原始方法几乎匹配的生成质量。

Conclusion: 端点预测无需轨迹拉直即可有效工作，为实现无需轨迹重新设计的推理加速提供了新的理论视角和实用方法。

Original Abstract: Diffusion and flow matching models generate high-quality samples, but their ODE samplers often need tens to hundreds of neural function evaluations (NFEs). This remains a practical challenge for released checkpoints, since many accelerators require additional design choices and training cost through retraining, distillation, or trajectory redesign. We investigate a different route based on $x$-prediction. During sampling, standard affine probability paths already expose $x_0$ information: an intermediate state and its path velocity determine a principled estimate of the clean sample. We formalize this property as \textbf{endpoint decodability} and show that the decoder is the minimum-MSE estimator $\mathbb{E}[x_0\mid x_t]$ under the usual $\ell_2$ objective. This yields \textbf{Truncated Jump Sampling} (TJS): stop the ODE at an early-exit time $t^*$ and return the decoded $x_0$. TJS requires no retraining, distillation, or architecture change. Across SDXL, SD3.5M, Z-Image-Turbo, and three class-conditional benchmarks, it reduces NFEs by 20--70\% with near-matched quality. The analysis also shows why endpoint prediction can work without straightening the trajectory, providing inference acceleration without trajectory redesign.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型和流匹配模型能生成高质量的样本，但其ODE采样器通常需要数十到数百次神经函数评估（NFE）。这对已发布的检查点而言仍是一个实际挑战，因为许多加速方法需要通过重新训练、蒸馏或轨迹重新设计来引入额外的设计选择和训练成本。我们研究了一种基于$x$预测的不同路线。在采样过程中，标准仿射概率路径已经暴露了$x_0$信息：一个中间状态及其路径速度可以推导出干净样本的原则性估计。我们将这一性质形式化为\textbf{端点可解码性}，并证明该解码器是在通常的$\ell_2$目标下$\mathbb{E}[x_0\mid x_t]$的最小MSE估计量。由此我们提出\textbf{截断跳跃采样}（TJS）：在早退时间$t^*$停止ODE，并返回解码后的$x_0$。TJS无需重新训练、蒸馏或架构更改。在SDXL、SD3.5M、Z-Image-Turbo以及三个类条件基准上，TJS将NFE减少了20-70%，同时生成质量几乎保持不变。分析还揭示了为何端点预测无需轨迹拉直即可有效工作，从而在不进行轨迹重新设计的情况下实现推理加速。

</details>


### [150] [Self-Supervised Implicit CEST Reconstruction via Physics-Informed Lorentz Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06132)
*Dexuan Li,Yupeng Wu,Chenglong Wang,Hanlin Liu,Hui Zhen,Jianqi Li,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理信息的洛伦兹编码（LE）框架，用于化学交换饱和转移（CEST）MRI的稀疏采样重建，通过隐式连续坐标学习将物理约束融入重建过程，显著提升重建质量并实现准确定量代谢物映射。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CEST MRI采集时间长，稀疏采样虽能减少扫描时间，但重建高质量Z谱是一个病态逆问题；传统插值和通用隐式神经表示缺乏物理约束，导致频谱伪影和物理上无效的信号。

Method: 提出洛伦兹编码（LE）框架：将CEST重建建模为自监督重建任务，通过隐式连续坐标学习实现；利用参数化洛伦兹型分布与可学习基函数组合，将稀疏坐标投影到物理约束空间中，正则化连续频谱映射，减少噪声并确保与物理模型一致。

Result: 在39点采样策略下，LE在活体人脑数据上达到PSNR 57.58 dB、SSIM 0.9994，显著优于现有方法；学习到的物理信息编码在潜空间中形成连续、几何有序的轨迹，确保准确的定量代谢物映射（APT、NOE、MT）。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹编码通过将物理约束嵌入隐式神经表示，有效解决了CEST稀疏重建中的病态逆问题，在保持高重建质量的同时实现物理一致性，为加速CEST MRI临床采集提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Multi-Pool Chemical Exchange Saturation Transfer (CEST) MRI provides valuable metabolic information but is clinically limited by long acquisition times. Although sparse sampling reduces scanning time, reconstructing high-resolution Z-spectra from limited data remains an ill-posed inverse problem. Conventional interpolation and generic Implicit Neural Rep-resentations (INRs) often lack physical constraints, leading to spectral artifacts and physically invalid signals. To address this, we propose Lorentz Encoding (LE), a physics-informed framework that formulates CEST reconstruction as a self-supervised reconstruction task via implicit continuous coordinate learning. Unlike generic positional encodings, LE regularizes the continuous spectral mapping by projecting sparse coordinates into a physically constrained space governed by a combination of parametric Lorentzian profiles with learnable basis functions. This mechanism effectively reduces noise and enforces consistency with physical models. Experiments on in vivo human brain data demonstrate that LE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Specifically, under a 39-point sampling strategy, LE achieves a PSNR of 57.58 dB and an SSIM of 0.9994. Furthermore, the learned physics-informed encodings form a continuous, geometrically ordered trajectory in the latent space, ensuring accurate quantitative metabo-lite mapping (APT, NOE, MT).

中文摘要翻译: 多池化学交换饱和转移（CEST）MRI能够提供有价值的代谢信息，但由于采集时间长而在临床应用中受到限制。尽管稀疏采样可以减少扫描时间，但从有限数据中重建高分辨率Z谱仍然是一个病态逆问题。传统的插值方法和通用隐式神经表示（INRs）通常缺乏物理约束，导致频谱伪影和物理上无效的信号。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了洛伦兹编码（Lorentz Encoding, LE），这是一个基于物理信息的框架，通过隐式连续坐标学习将CEST重建建模为自监督重建任务。与通用的位置编码不同，LE通过将稀疏坐标投影到一个由参数化洛伦兹型分布与可学习基函数组合所约束的物理空间中，来正则化连续频谱映射。该机制有效降低了噪声，并强制结果与物理模型保持一致。在活体人脑数据上的实验表明，LE显著优于现有最先进方法。具体而言，在39点采样策略下，LE达到了57.58 dB的PSNR和0.9994的SSIM。此外，学习到的物理信息编码在潜空间中形成了一条连续的、几何有序的轨迹，确保了准确的定量代谢物映射（APT、NOE、MT）。

</details>


### [151] [Leveraging Extragradient for Effective Sharpness-Aware Minimization in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06151)
*Yao Fu,Chunxia Zhang,Junmin Liu,Yihang Jin,Haishan Ye,Yuanao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于外梯度技术的锐度感知最小化优化器（EISAM），通过两步更新过程（预测步+扰动步）寻找损失平面的平坦最小值，相比SAM、SGD和Adam显著提升泛化性能，并降低了对扰动半径的敏感性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统优化器（如SGD）倾向于收敛到尖锐最小值，导致过拟合和泛化性能下降；虽然SAM通过寻找平坦最小值改善了泛化，但仍有进一步改进空间，特别是在减少超参数敏感性和提升泛化界方面。

Method: 提出EISAM，它采用两步更新过程：第一步是预测步，探索损失景观的几何结构；第二步是扰动步，结合基础优化器精炼更新。该方法受外梯度技术启发，在SAM框架基础上引入额外梯度信息以更有效地引导参数向平坦最小值方向优化。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，EISAM在测试准确率和训练效率上一致优于SGD、Adam和SAM；理论分析证明EISAM通过引导参数向曲率更小的平坦最小值移动，收紧了泛化界；此外EISAM显著降低了对扰动半径的敏感度，简化了超参数调优。

Conclusion: EISAM是一种鲁棒、可扩展且广泛适用的深度学习优化方案，在理论和实践上均推动了深度学习优化的发展，为实际调优提供了实用指导。

Original Abstract: Generalization remains a pivotal challenge in deep learning, where traditional optimizers like Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) often converge to sharp minima, leading to overfitting and reduced performance on unseen data. Building on Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM), for seeking flat minima associated with improved generalization, we propose the Extragradient-Inspired Sharpness-Aware Minimization (EISAM), a novel optimizer that enhances generalization via the extragradient technique. EISAM uses a two-step update process: a prediction step investigating the geometry of the loss landscape and a perturbation step that refines updates with a base optimizer. This approach achieves better generalization performance than SAM. Crucially, EISAM reduces sensitivity to the perturbation radius, enhancing robustness, and simplifying the tuning across diverse settings. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that EISAM consistently outperforms SGD, Adaptive Moment Estimation (Adam), and SAM in test accuracy and training efficiency across various architectures. Theoretical analysis further confirms that EISAM tightens the generalization bound by steering parameters toward flatter minima with reduced curvature. Accompanied by a thorough hyperparameter analysis, EISAM offers practical tuning guidance, establishing it as a robust, scalable, and broadly applicable optimization solution that advances both the theory and practice in deep learning.

中文摘要翻译: 泛化仍然是深度学习中的一个关键挑战，传统的优化器如随机梯度下降（SGD）往往收敛到尖锐最小值，导致过拟合并在未见数据上性能下降。基于锐度感知最小化（SAM）方法——该方法旨在寻找与改善泛化相关的平坦最小值——我们提出了外梯度启发的锐度感知最小化（EISAM），一种通过外梯度技术增强泛化性能的新型优化器。EISAM采用两步更新过程：第一步是预测步，用于探索损失景观的几何结构；第二步是扰动步，结合基础优化器对更新进行精炼。该方法比SAM实现了更好的泛化性能。关键在于，EISAM降低了对扰动半径的敏感性，增强了鲁棒性，并简化了在不同设置下的超参数调优。在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，EISAM在各种架构下的测试准确率和训练效率上均一致优于SGD、自适应矩估计（Adam）和SAM。理论分析进一步证实，EISAM通过引导参数向曲率更小的平坦最小值移动，收紧了泛化界。结合全面的超参数分析，EISAM提供了实用的调优指导，成为了一种鲁棒、可扩展且广泛适用的优化解决方案，推动了深度学习理论与实践的发展。

</details>


### [152] [X-FEMR: A Token-level Explainable Approach for Electronic Health Records Foundation Models using Transformer-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06163)
*Jie Huang,Pengfei Yin,Zihan Xu,Daniel Capurro,Mike Conway,Ting Dang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个面向电子健康记录基础模型（FEMR）的token级可解释性方法，通过训练基于Transformer的代理模型来近似FEMR行为，并引入临床对齐度量评估解释的临床相关性，实验表明该方法在保持预测性能的同时提供了符合临床知识的可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有FEMR虽然有效，但属于黑箱模型，引发了关于偏见、可解释性和临床信任的担忧，因此需要一种能够揭示FEMR决策过程的可解释性方法。

Method: 训练一个基于Transformer的代理模型，在两项预测任务上使用FEMR的输入-输出对进行训练，以近似FEMR行为并保留时间动态；然后识别最具影响力的token；并引入临床对齐度量来量化代理模型的关键token与临床验证特征之间的对应关系。

Result: 代理模型能够紧密近似FEMR的预测结果，且token级解释与临床知识具有良好的一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法为可解释且值得信赖的临床AI提供了实用框架。

Original Abstract: Foundation Models for Electronic Health Records (FEMRs) are pretrained on large-scale structured patient data, enabling them to convert longitudinal patient trajectories into generalizable representations for diverse clinical prediction tasks. Despite their effectiveness, FEMRs remain black-box models, raising concerns about bias, interpretability, and clinical trust. To address this, we propose the first token-level explainability approach for FEMRs. We train a Transformer-based surrogate model on input-output pairs from the FEMR across two prediction tasks, approximating its behavior while preserving temporal dynamics. We identify the most influential tokens, providing insights into how FEMRs leverage different aspects of patient history for predictions. To evaluate clinical relevance, we introduce a novel clinical alignment metric that quantifies the correspondence between the surrogate model's key tokens and clinically validated features. Our results demonstrate that the surrogate closely approximates FEMR predictions and that token-level explanations align well with clinical knowledge, offering a practical framework for interpretable and trustworthy clinical AI.

中文摘要翻译: 电子健康记录基础模型（FEMRs）在大规模结构化患者数据上进行预训练，能够将纵向患者轨迹转化为适用于多种临床预测任务的通用表示。尽管效果显著，FEMRs仍然是黑箱模型，引发了关于偏见、可解释性和临床信任的担忧。为解决这一问题，我们提出了首个面向FEMR的token级可解释性方法。我们在两项预测任务上，基于FEMR的输入-输出对训练了一个基于Transformer的代理模型，在保留时间动态的同时近似其行为。我们识别出最具影响力的token，揭示了FEMR如何利用患者历史的不同方面进行预测。为评估临床相关性，我们引入了一种新的临床对齐度量，量化代理模型的关键token与临床验证特征之间的对应关系。结果表明，代理模型能够紧密近似FEMR的预测结果，且token级解释与临床知识具有良好的对齐性，为可解释且值得信赖的临床AI提供了实用框架。

</details>


### [153] [Canopy: A Heterograph Foundation Model for Metabolic Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06224)
*Jake Bowden,Laurence Legon,Satnam Surae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Canopy是一个异构图表征学习基础模型，整合多源生物数据，通过预训练异构图表征在发酵滴度预测任务上优于传统方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有代谢工程计算方法要么依赖无法从实验数据学习的 stoichiometric 约束模型，要么使用丢弃生物知识关系结构的表格机器学习，缺乏能够整合多源异质数据并捕捉生物学关系结构的学习方法。

Method: 构建包含690万节点、13种节点类型和34种边类型的统一知识图谱，使用ESM-2、MoLFormer和PubMedBERT编码节点特征，采用加入SignNet位置编码、Jumping Knowledge聚合和虚拟节点的异构图表征（HGT）进行预训练，通过链接预测、掩码节点建模、距离预测和对比实验聚类四个自监督目标联合优化。

Result: 在发酵滴度预测下游任务中，冻结的Canopy嵌入结合轻量分类器达到$R^2=0.41$，优于表格基线（最优$R^2=0.24$）和同质GNN变体。

Conclusion: 异构图表征基础模型能够有效整合多源生物数据，在代谢工程关键预测任务上显著优于传统方法，展示了图结构在捕捉生物知识关系中的优势。

Original Abstract: Designing microbial strains that produce high-value chemicals at commercially viable titers remains a central challenge in metabolic engineering. Existing computational approaches either rely on stoichiometric constraint-based models that cannot learn from experimental data, or apply tabular machine learning to hand-crafted features that discard the relational structure of biological knowledge. We present Canopy, a heterogeneous graph foundation model that integrates ten public and proprietary data sources into a unified knowledge graph (KG) of 6.9M nodes across 13 types and 34 edge types, covering genes, proteins, metabolites, reactions, pathways, strains, and fermentation experiments. Node features are encoded through domain-specific foundation models (ESM-2 for protein sequences, MoLFormer for chemical SMILES, and PubMedBERT for biomedical text), yielding a multi-modal representation within a single graph. We pretrain a Heterogeneous Graph Transformer (HGT) augmented with SignNet positional encodings, Jumping Knowledge aggregation, and virtual nodes using four self-supervised objectives (link prediction, masked node modelling, distance prediction, and contrastive experiment clustering), balanced via learned homoscedastic uncertainty weighting. On the downstream task of fermentation titer prediction, frozen Canopy embeddings achieve $R^{2} = 0.41$ with a lightweight probe, outperforming tabular baselines (best $R^{2} = 0.24$) and homogeneous GNN variants.

中文摘要翻译: 设计能够以商业可行滴度生产高价值化学品的微生物菌株仍然是代谢工程的核心挑战。现有的计算方法要么依赖无法从实验数据学习的化学计量约束模型，要么将表格机器学习应用于丢弃生物知识关系结构的手工特征。我们提出了Canopy，一个异构图表征基础模型，它将十个公共和专有数据源整合为一个包含690万个节点、13种节点类型和34种边类型的统一知识图谱，覆盖基因、蛋白质、代谢物、反应、通路、菌株和发酵实验。节点特征通过领域特定的基础模型（蛋白质序列用ESM-2，化学SMILES用MoLFormer，生物医学文本用PubMedBERT）编码，在单个图中形成多模态表征。我们预训练了一个异构图表征（HGT），并增强了SignNet位置编码、Jumping Knowledge聚合和虚拟节点，使用四个自监督目标（链接预测、掩码节点建模、距离预测和对比实验聚类），通过学习的异方差不确定性权重进行平衡。在发酵滴度预测的下游任务上，冻结的Canopy嵌入结合轻量分类器达到$R^2=0.41$，优于表格基线（最优$R^2=0.24$）和同质GNN变体。

</details>


### [154] [Quantitative Gaussian-Process limits of Tensor Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06290)
*Andrea Agazzi,Eloy Mosig García,Dario Trevisan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从张量程序角度研究了随机神经网络在无限宽时的高斯过程极限，并给出了Wasserstein距离下的定量收敛理论，主要结果是在有限宽度下显式误差界与宽度平方根成反比，该框架与架构无关，适用于前馈、循环和Transformer等模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为随机神经网络在无限宽极限下的高斯过程收敛性提供定量化的理论保证，填补现有理论在有限宽度误差估计和架构通用性方面的空白。

Method: 采用张量程序框架，通过分析神经网络的张量计算结构，推导出有限宽度网络执行与其高斯过程极限之间的Wasserstein距离误差界。

Result: 得到了显式的有限宽度误差界，误差阶为宽度平方根的倒数(O(1/√width))，且该结果适用于前馈网络、循环网络和Transformer等多种架构。

Conclusion: 从张量程序视角统一了多种架构下随机神经网络的高斯过程极限理论，并提供了非渐近的定量收敛保证。

Original Abstract: We study the infinite-width Gaussian-process limit of random neural networks
  through the lens of tensor programs, and we provide a quantitative convergence
  theory in Wasserstein distance.
  Our main result gives explicit finite-width error bounds, of order inverse square-root of the widths
  between finite-network executions and their
  Gaussian-process limits. The framework is architecture-agnostic and covers feed-forward models together
  with weight-sharing schemes relevant for recurrent and transformer-type
  architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 我们从张量程序的角度研究了随机神经网络在无限宽极限下的高斯过程极限，并提供了Wasserstein距离下的定量收敛理论。我们的主要结果给出了显式的有限宽度误差界，阶数为宽度平方根的倒数，该误差界介于有限网络执行与其高斯过程极限之间。该框架与架构无关，涵盖了前馈模型以及适用于循环和Transformer类型架构的权重共享方案。

</details>


### [155] [Physics-Informed Neural Embeddings of PDE Solution Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06348)
*Raul Jimenez,Svitlana Mayboroda,Pavlos Protopapas,Leonid Sarieddine,David N. Spergel,Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息的框架，用于学习偏微分方程解族的有限维嵌入。该方法使用多头物理信息神经网络，通过头部正交化惩罚去除潜在表示中的退化，并发现Burgers方程等问题的解流形具有显著的有效维度降低（2-4个主成分即可捕获约95%的方差）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统方法学习PDE解空间通常需要高维表示，缺乏对解流形几何结构的可解释性分析。本文旨在学习解空间的低维嵌入，并建立稳健的可观测特征来刻画解流形的几何性质。

Method: 采用多头物理信息神经网络（multihead PINN），共享体网络学习表示解空间的潜在流形，线性头网络重建不同初始条件下的解。引入头部正交化惩罚消除潜在表示的退化，稳定主成分谱。通过傅里叶壳（wavenumber bands）分析每个主成分的频率贡献分布。

Result: 对一维粘性Burgers方程，潜在维度nb=20时，2-4个主成分捕获约95%的潜在空间方差，4-7个捕获约99%；热方程和波动方程表现出类似的压缩特性。傅里叶壳频率分布在潜在空间基变换下保持不变，在不同训练运行间具有可重复性。

Conclusion: 学习到的谱分布和主成分可作为解流形几何的稳健可观测量，为理解PDE解空间的内在低维结构提供了新的分析工具。

Original Abstract: We introduce a physics-informed framework for learning finite-dimensional embeddings of solution families of partial differential equations. The method uses a multihead Physics-Informed Neural Network in which a shared body learns a latent manifold representing the solution space, while linear heads reconstruct individual solutions associated with different initial conditions. A head-orthogonalization penalty removes degeneracies in the latent representation and stabilizes the principal-component spectrum across training realizations. Because the initial condition is built into the network output by construction, these principal components measure the additional variability the network learns on top of the initial profile, not the full solution itself. We apply the method to the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation, with the heat and wave equations as robustness checks. For a latent dimension $n_b=20$, the learned manifolds exhibit pronounced effective dimensional reduction: for Burgers dynamics, only $2$-$4$ principal components capture about $95\%$ of the latent-space variance, while $4$-$7$ capture about $99\%$, depending on the initial-condition family; the same qualitative compression holds for the heat and wave equations. We also split the wavenumber axis into bands (``Fourier shells'') and measure how much each band contributes to every principal component. The resulting frequency profile is invariant under the change-of-basis freedom that the orthogonalization penalty leaves in the latent space, and is therefore reproducible across independent training runs. More broadly, this establishes the learned spectral profiles and principal components as robust observables of solution-manifold geometry.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一个基于物理信息的框架，用于学习偏微分方程解族的有限维嵌入。该方法采用多头物理信息神经网络，其中共享体网络学习表示解空间的潜在流形，而线性头网络则重建与不同初始条件对应的各个解。头部正交化惩罚消除了潜在表示中的退化，并稳定了不同训练实现中的主成分谱。由于初始条件被直接构建到网络输出中，这些主成分衡量的是网络在初始轮廓之上额外学习到的变异性，而非完整解本身。我们将该方法应用于一维粘性Burgers方程，并以热方程和波动方程作为鲁棒性检验。对于潜在维度nb=20，学习到的流形表现出显著的有效降维：对于Burgers动力学，仅2-4个主成分即可捕获约95%的潜在空间方差，而4-7个可捕获约99%（具体取决于初始条件族）；热方程和波动方程也呈现出相同的定性压缩特性。我们还将波数轴划分为若干频带（"傅里叶壳"），并测量每个频带对每个主成分的贡献程度。由此产生的频率分布在正交化惩罚在潜在空间中保留的基变换自由度下保持不变，因此在独立训练运行间具有可重复性。更广泛地说，这确立了学习到的谱分布和主成分作为解流形几何的稳健可观测量。

</details>


### [156] [TILDE: TILt-based Distributional Erasure for Concept Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06432)
*Naveen George,Naoki Murata,Yuhta Takida,Konda Reddy Mopuri,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TILDE方法，将概念遗忘形式化为分布对齐问题，通过能量倾斜的最小偏差条件分布作为目标，并利用残差∇-GFlowNet训练来学习分数校正，在保持良性生成质量的同时实现有效的概念遗忘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型的概念遗忘方法虽然能有效移除目标概念，但往往忽视了对良性生成的质量、多样性和语义覆盖度的保持；且常见擦除目标未明确指定后遗忘分布应如何逼近理想参考分布（即从头训练的不含目标数据的保留模型），导致保留能力只是更新规则的隐含结果而非显式目标。

Method: 提出TILDE（基于倾斜的分布擦除）：(1) 将概念遗忘形式化为分布对齐问题，目标是在遗忘约束下与预训练模型的最小偏差条件分布，即能量倾斜的锚点自由目标；(2) 该目标抑制表达目标概念的图像，同时保留每个提示下良性样本的相对质量；(3) 通过残差∇-GFlowNet训练来实例化该原则，学习由遗忘能量引起的相对于预训练扩散模型的分数校正。

Result: 在物体、艺术风格和角色等多个概念遗忘任务上，TILDE实现了强遗忘效果，同时在保留能力和分布保真度上优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: TILDE通过将概念遗忘重新定义为具有显式保留目标的分布对齐问题，有效平衡了概念移除与良性生成质量保持之间的矛盾，为扩散模型的安全部署提供了更实用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Concept unlearning in text-to-image diffusion models is critical for safe and practical deployment: with rising privacy concerns, copyright disputes, trademark constraints, and safety regulations, deployed systems must be able to suppress unwanted concepts after training. Existing methods often remove the target concept effectively, but practical unlearning also requires an equally fundamental property: the unlearned model should retain quality, diversity, and semantic coverage on benign generation. The gold standard is a retain-only model trained from scratch without the unwanted data. However, common erasure objectives do not specify which post-unlearning distribution should approximate this reference, leaving retention as an implicit consequence of the update rule. We propose TILDE, TILt-based Distributional Erasure, which formulates concept unlearning as a distributional alignment problem: the desired target is the minimum-deviation conditional distribution from the pretrained model under a forgetting constraint. This energy-tilted, anchor-free target suppresses concept-expressing images while preserving benign relative mass for each prompt. We instantiate this principle with residual $\nabla$-GFlowNet training, which learns the score correction induced by the forget energy relative to the pretrained diffusion model. Across objects, artistic styles, and characters, TILDE achieves strong forgetting while improving retention and distributional fidelity over prior baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像扩散模型中的概念遗忘对于安全且实际部署至关重要：随着隐私问题、版权纠纷、商标限制和安全法规的日益突出，已部署系统必须能够在训练后抑制不需要的概念。现有方法通常能有效移除目标概念，但实际遗忘还需要一个同样基本的特性：遗忘模型应在良性生成上保持质量、多样性和语义覆盖度。黄金标准是从头训练一个不含目标数据的纯保留模型。然而，常见的擦除目标并未明确指定后遗忘分布应如何逼近这一参考分布，使得保留能力只是更新规则的隐含结果。我们提出TILDE，即基于倾斜的分布擦除（TILt-based Distributional Erasure），它将概念遗忘形式化为一个分布对齐问题：期望的目标是在遗忘约束下与预训练模型的最小偏差条件分布。这个能量倾斜的、无锚点目标在抑制表达目标概念图像的同时，为每个提示保留了良性样本的相对概率质量。我们通过残差∇-GFlowNet训练来实例化这一原则，该训练学习由遗忘能量引起的相对于预训练扩散模型的分数校正。在物体、艺术风格和角色等多个任务上，TILDE实现了强遗忘效果，同时在保留能力和分布保真度上优于现有基线方法。

</details>


### [157] [EntroPath: Maximum Entropy Path Ensemble Embedding for Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06497)
*Przemysław Rola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EntroPath 是一种通过最大熵随机游走的路径集成来恢复数据图中测地线距离的流形学习方法，在非均匀采样和分支轨迹场景下优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于图的嵌入方法（如局部归一化随机游走或最短路径距离）存在缺陷：前者会导致扩散在稠密采样区域集中，后者对图中的伪捷径边敏感。因此需要一种既能避免局部密度偏差又不受虚假边干扰的流形学习方法。

Method: 基于最大熵随机游走（MERW）构建不相似性度量，通过聚合两点间所有k步路径的完整集成而非单条轨迹；利用Varadhan热核公式证明短时间极限下自由能不相似性收敛到平方测地线距离；扩散深度k可平滑插值局部与全局几何；通过对称核进行精确格拉姆分解，并提供了地标投影和扩散势伪时间等可扩展扩展。

Result: 在合成流形和单细胞基准上，EntroPath 一致地匹配或优于基于扩散和最短路径的方法，在局部结构指标上与UMAP、t-SNE等邻域保持嵌入方法竞争力相当；在非均匀采样密度和良好分离的分支轨迹流形上优势最为显著。

Conclusion: 路径集成扩散比单一轨迹或最短路径更忠实地保留了底层测地线几何，EntroPath 为图嵌入提供了一种有效的替代方案。

Original Abstract: We introduce EntroPath, a manifold learning method that recovers geodesic geometry from data graphs through ensembles of diffusion paths. Many existing graph-based embeddings rely either on locally normalised random walks or on shortest-path distances. The former can concentrate diffusion in densely sampled regions, while the latter are sensitive to spurious shortcut edges in the graph. EntroPath instead builds its dissimilarities from the maximum entropy random walk (MERW), which aggregates the full ensemble of k-step paths between points rather than relying on any single trajectory. We show that the resulting free-energy dissimilarity converges to squared geodesic distance in the short-time limit, via Varadhan's heat-kernel formula. The diffusion depth k interpolates smoothly between local neighbourhood structure and global manifold geometry, and the symmetrised kernel admits an exact Gram factorisation connecting EntroPath to kernel methods. We further provide scalable extensions via landmark projection and diffusion-potential pseudotime. Across synthetic manifolds and single-cell benchmarks, EntroPath consistently matches or outperforms diffusion- and shortest-path-based methods, while remaining competitive with neighbourhood-preserving embeddings (UMAP, t-SNE) on local-structure metrics. Its gains are most pronounced on manifolds with non-uniform sampling density and well-separated branching trajectories, where path-ensemble diffusion more faithfully preserves the underlying geodesic geometry.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了 EntroPath，一种通过扩散路径集成从数据图中恢复测地线几何的流形学习方法。许多现有的基于图的嵌入要么依赖局部归一化随机游走，要么依赖最短路径距离。前者会导致扩散在密集采样区域集中，而后者对图中的伪捷径边敏感。EntroPath 则基于最大熵随机游走（MERW）构建不相似性度量，该游走聚合了点之间所有k步路径的完整集成，而非依赖单条轨迹。我们证明了通过 Varadhan 热核公式，在短时间极限下，得到的自由能不相似性收敛到平方测地线距离。扩散深度k在局部邻域结构和全局流形几何之间平滑插值，且对称核允许精确的格拉姆分解，将 EntroPath 与核方法联系起来。我们进一步通过地标投影和扩散势伪时间提供了可扩展的扩展。在合成流形和单细胞基准上，EntroPath 一致地匹配或优于基于扩散和最短路径的方法，同时在局部结构指标上与邻域保持嵌入方法（UMAP、t-SNE）保持竞争力。其优势在具有非均匀采样密度和良好分离的分支轨迹的流形上最为显著，其中路径集成扩散更忠实地保留了底层测地线几何。

</details>


### [158] [GraphBU: MILP Instance Generation with Graph-Native Block Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06532)
*Xiaolei Guo,Chenyu Zhou,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GraphBU，一种以局部子问题及其接口为基本单元的图原生MILP生成器，通过提升耦合节点为约束或边界变量来保证结构一致性，生成实例在图统计、可行性和下游任务上均有提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MILP生成器以公式模板、统计摘要、局部图编辑或重组合的块为生成单元，未能显式记录局部子问题与实例其余部分的耦合关系，导致生成实例难以保持求解器和学习策略依赖的结构。

Method: 提出GraphBU，一种图原生生成器，基本单元为局部子问题及其接口。通过将耦合节点提升为主约束或边界变量来分离接口，得到块单元；基于接口-松弛条件进行兼容性检查后替换，生成新实例。该方法在置换不变性下构建图结构。

Result: 在图统计相似性上平均约0.934，可行性约96.7%，下游Predict-and-Search训练的主指标提升约8.0%。

Conclusion: GraphBU能够更好地保留MILP的结构特性，生成实例保持高可行性，并有效提升下游学习任务的性能。

Original Abstract: Mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) instances used for solver development are hard to obtain when models come from private or application-specific pipelines. A generator must keep the structure that solvers and learned policies rely on. Existing general generators usually choose their generation unit from a formulation template, summary statistics, local graph edits, or blocks found after recombination. These units do not explicitly record how a local part of the MILP is coupled to the rest of the instance. We propose GraphBU, a graph-native generator whose basic unit is a local subproblem plus its interface. The method promotes coupling nodes into master constraints or boundary variables and uses the resulting block units for compatibility-checked replacement. The analysis focuses on the properties needed by this construction: promotion separates interfaces, replacement can preserve feasibility under an interface-slack condition, and the graph construction is invariant to row-column permutations. On MILP instances generation, this unit keeps graph statistics close to the source family, preserves feasibility on most datasets, and improves downstream Predict-and-Search training. Genrated by GraphBU, The average graph-statistical similarity was approximately 0.934, the average feasibility was approximately 96.7%, and the average increase in the main index of downstream PS was approximately 8.0%.

中文摘要翻译: 用于求解器开发的混合整数线性规划（MILP）实例在模型来自私有或特定应用流水线时难以获取。生成器必须保持求解器和学习策略所依赖的结构。现有的通用生成器通常从公式模板、汇总统计、局部图编辑或重组后发现的块中选择其生成单元。这些单元没有显式记录MILP的局部部分与实例其余部分之间的耦合方式。我们提出GraphBU，一种图原生的生成器，其基本单元是一个局部子问题及其接口。该方法将耦合节点提升为主约束或边界变量，并利用生成的块单元进行兼容性检查的替换。分析聚焦于这一构造所需的性质：提升分离了接口，替换能够在接口松弛条件下保持可行性，且图构造对行列置换具有不变性。在MILP实例生成中，该单元使图统计量接近源族，在大多数数据集上保持可行性，并改进了下游的Predict-and-Search训练。由GraphBU生成的实例，平均图统计相似度约为0.934，平均可行性约为96.7%，下游PS主要指标的平均提升约为8.0%。

</details>


### [159] [Graph Convolutional Attention: A Spectral Perspective on Graph Denoising and Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06546)
*Shervin Khalafi,Igor Krawczuk,Sergio Rozada,Charilaos Kanatsoulis,Antonio G Marques,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了线性注意力在去噪目标下只能学习平均谱滤波，存在根本性局限；为解决该问题，提出了谱注意力（Spectral Attention）及其实践版本图卷积注意力（GCA），通过利用输入图谱实现自适应去噪，理论分析和实验均表明其优于线性注意力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于注意力的图去噪方法（尤其是线性注意力）在理论上缺乏深入理解，难以确定标准注意力机制是否适合图去噪任务。

Method: 提出谱注意力（Spectral Attention），直接利用输入图谱实现去噪；并推导出图卷积注意力（GCA），通过图滤波的查询和键实现谱去噪，且具有置换等变性。

Result: 线性注意力只能学习平均谱滤波；谱注意力理论上优于线性注意力，差距由谱多样性决定；softmax操作可额外提供去噪效果；实验表明GCA在图去噪和扩散任务上一致优于线性注意力，在DiGress中无需昂贵结构特征即可匹配标准图Transformer性能。

Conclusion: 线性注意力在谱域存在根本性局限，所提出的谱注意力和GCA能有效克服该局限，为注意力机制在图去噪中的设计提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: Denoising graphs is a fundamental problem in graph learning and the core operation of graph diffusion models. Attention-based architectures like graph transformers have recently shown promise in denoising graphs. However, our principled understanding of attention-based graph denoising remains limited, making it unclear whether standard attention is the right mechanism for this task. Here we show that, under a denoising objective, linear attention is suboptimal and can only learn an average spectral denoising filter over the training distribution. This creates a fundamental limitation as graphs often vary spectrally across the distribution. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Spectral Attention, which directly utilizes the input graph spectrum and provably outperforms linear attention by a margin governed by the spectral diversity of the distribution. We then derive Graph Convolutional Attention (GCA), a practical and permutation-equivariant realization of this idea that implements spectral denoising through graph-filtered queries and keys. For stochastic block models, GCA provably matches the idealized Spectral Attention mechanism. We further show that the softmax operation, that follows the attention, provides additional denoising by approximately projecting noisy eigenvectors onto the clean eigenspace. Empirically, replacing linear attention with GCA consistently improves graph denoising and diffusion on synthetic and real datasets, with gains strongly correlated with spectral diversity. In DiGress, GCA matches standard graph-transformer performance without computing expensive structural features, and when combined with the recently proposed PEARL positional encodings, avoids explicit eigendecomposition computations resulting in faster inference without degrading quality. The code can be found here: github.com/shervinkhalafi/graph_conv_att

中文摘要翻译: 图去噪是图学习中的一个基础问题，也是图扩散模型的核心操作。最近，基于注意力的架构（如图Transformer）在图去噪中展现出了潜力。然而，我们对基于注意力的图去噪的原理性理解仍然有限，这导致尚不清楚标准注意力是否是完成此任务的正确机制。本文表明，在去噪目标下，线性注意力是次优的，且只能学习训练分布上的平均谱去噪滤波器。这带来了一个根本性局限，因为图在分布上的谱往往存在差异。为了克服这一局限，我们引入了谱注意力（Spectral Attention），它直接利用输入图谱，并理论上证明其性能优于线性注意力，优势程度由分布的谱多样性决定。随后，我们推导出图卷积注意力（Graph Convolutional Attention, GCA），它是该思想的一个实用且具有置换等变性的实现，通过图滤波的查询和键来实现谱去噪。对于随机块模型，GCA理论上能够匹配理想化的谱注意力机制。我们进一步表明，注意力之后的softmax操作通过将噪声特征向量近似投影到干净特征空间上，提供了额外的去噪效果。在实验上，将线性注意力替换为GCA在合成和真实数据集上一致地改进了图去噪和扩散性能，且增益与谱多样性高度相关。在DiGress中，GCA在不计算昂贵结构特征的情况下即可匹配标准图Transformer的性能；当与最近提出的PEARL位置编码结合时，避免了显式的特征分解计算，从而在不降低质量的情况下实现了更快的推理。代码可在以下地址获取：github.com/shervinkhalafi/graph_conv_att

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Replicating the Signature: Unsupervised Targeted Impersonation Attack on RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05549)
*Haytham Albousayri,Bechir Hamdaoui*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的冒充攻击框架，利用无监督学习估计目标设备的硬件特定缺陷，并结合信号处理生成高保真对抗信号，以欺骗RF指纹识别系统。实验表明，在严格访问限制下，该攻击在BLE设备测试中针对性的攻击成功率超过现有基线80%以上。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RF指纹识别系统容易受到对抗性攻击，但此前攻击方法通常假设攻击者完全访问合法（受害者）RFFP分类器，这在实际场景中不现实。本文旨在设计一种更现实的攻击策略，使攻击者从完全不同的收发器硬件上发动攻击，无需访问目标分类器。

Method: 提出了一种结合无监督学习与信号处理的对抗攻击框架。首先利用无监督学习对目标设备的硬件特定缺陷（impairments）进行准确估计；然后基于信号处理的方法合成高保真的对抗信号，模仿目标设备的RF指纹特征。

Result: 在BLE设备测试平台上，该攻击在视距（LoS）和非视距（NLoS）场景下均表现高效，在严格访问约束下针对性的攻击成功率超过现有基线80%以上。此外，分析了跨域泛化、信号表示不匹配和分类器多样性对攻击效果的影响，证明了攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的攻击框架在更现实的攻击假设下（从不同硬件发动攻击、无需访问目标分类器）依然能高效欺骗RF指纹识别系统，揭示了现有RFFP系统在面对硬件缺陷模仿攻击时的脆弱性。

Original Abstract: This paper presents a novel impersonation attack framework that aims to fool RF Fingerprinting (RFFP) identification systems by synthesizing signals that replicate the hardware-specific impairments of a target device. Our framework leverages unsupervised learning to enable accurate impairment estimation, combined with signal processing-based generation to synthesize high-fidelity adversarial signals. Unlike prior works that assume full access to the legitimate (victim) RFFP classifier, we consider a more realistic attack strategy where the adversary performs the attack from a completely different transceiver hardware. We further evaluate our proposed attack under realistic and challenging deployment settings, including over-the-air transmission in both Line-of-Sight (LoS) and Non-Line-of-Sight (NLoS) scenarios. Extensive experiments conducted on a Bluetooth Low Energy (BLE) device testbed demonstrate that our attacks remain highly effective even under severe access constraints, significantly outperforming existing baselines in terms of targeted attack success rates by over 80%. We additionally analyze the effects of cross-domain generalization, signal representation mismatch, and classifier diversity, highlighting the robustness and

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种新颖的冒充攻击框架，旨在通过合成复制目标设备硬件特定缺陷的信号来欺骗射频指纹识别系统。该框架利用无监督学习实现准确的缺陷估计，并结合基于信号处理的生成方法合成高保真对抗信号。与以往假设完全访问合法（受害者）RFFP分类器的工作不同，我们考虑了一种更现实的攻击策略，即攻击者从完全不同的收发器硬件上发动攻击。我们进一步在现实且具有挑战性的部署设置下评估了所提出的攻击，包括视距和非视距场景下的空中传输。在蓝牙低功耗设备测试平台上进行的大量实验表明，即使在严格的访问约束下，我们的攻击仍保持高效，在针对性攻击成功率上显著超过现有基线80%以上。我们还分析了跨域泛化、信号表示不匹配和分类器多样性的影响，突显了攻击的鲁棒性和有效性。

</details>


### [161] [DBNN: Neural Spike Classification Using a Deep Binarized Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05590)
*Binyi Ren,Luca M. Meyer,Majid Zamani*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向硬件实现的深度二值化神经网络（DBNN）尖峰分类系统，用于植入式脑机接口中的片上尖峰排序。该系统采用二值化隐藏层和定点输出层，实现无乘法器推理，在合成和体内数据集上达到98.7%的中位分类精度，FPGA原型仅消耗828个ALMs和1023个寄存器，ASIC实现面积0.014 mm²，功耗122 nW（20 kHz），非常适合低功耗植入式神经接口。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 植入式脑机接口需要在节点上进行片上尖峰排序，以降低遥测带宽和功耗，同时保持可靠的神经解码能力。传统尖峰排序方法硬件开销大，亟需一种硬件友好、高精度且低功耗的解决方案。

Method: 提出硬件导向的深度二值化神经网络（DBNN），包含两个二值化隐藏层（各256神经元）和一个定点输出层，推理过程以符号控制累加和按位逻辑为主，无需乘法器。网络输入为紧凑的16样本尖峰波形，架构为16-256-256-3。

Result: 在合成和体内数据集上达到98.7%的中位分类精度。FPGA原型（Cyclone V，50 MHz）每尖峰528周期，计算延迟0.01 ms，消耗828 ALMs和1023个寄存器，零DSP块。ASIC实现（FreePDK45）面积0.014 mm²，功耗122 nW（20 kHz，1.1 V供电）。

Conclusion: 提出的DBNN尖峰分类器在精度与硬件开销之间实现了良好权衡，以极低的硬件代价（0.014 mm²、122 nW）达到98.7%的高精度，且无需乘法器，非常适合低功耗植入式神经接口。这是首个专为实时神经尖峰排序设计的DBNN，在输入数据大小与网络复杂度之间取得了出色平衡。

Original Abstract: Implantable brain-computer interfaces require on-node spike sorting to reduce telemetry bandwidth and power while maintaining reliable neural decoding. This paper presents a hardware-oriented deep binarized neural network (DBNN) spike-sorting system with two binarized hidden layers with 256 neurons and a fixed-point output layer to enable multiplier-free inference dominated by sign-controlled accumulation and bit-wise logic. The proposed classifier operates on compact 16-sample spike waveforms to reduce the implementation cost (16-256-256-3) and achieves a median classification accuracy of 98.7% on both synthetic and in-vivo datasets. An FPGA prototype on a Cyclone V device operates at 50 MHz and requires 528 cycles per spike, corresponding to a 0.01 ms compute latency, while consuming 828 ALMs and 1023 registers with zero DSP blocks. For ASIC feasibility, the DBNN is implemented using FreePDK45-based flow; synthesis in Synopsys Design Compiler indicates an estimated silicon area of 0.014 mm2 and an operating power of 122 nW at 20 kHz under a 1.1 V supply. These results demonstrate that the proposed DBNN spike sorter offers a favorable trade-off between accuracy and implementation cost, supporting low-power, implantable neural interfaces. Overall, the proposed DBNN spike sorter achieves high accuracy (98.7%) with extremely low hardware cost (0.014 mm2, 122 nW at 20 kHz) and multiplier-free operation, making it suitable for low-power, implantable neural interfaces. This paper introduces the first DBNN designed for real-time neural spike sorting, striking an excellent balance between input data size and network complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 植入式脑机接口需要片上尖峰排序来降低遥测带宽和功耗，同时保持可靠的神经解码。本文提出了一种面向硬件的深度二值化神经网络（DBNN）尖峰分类系统，包含两个分别有256个神经元的二值化隐藏层和一个定点输出层，从而实现了以符号控制累加和按位逻辑为主的无乘法器推理。所提出的分类器处理紧凑的16样本尖峰波形以降低实现成本（16-256-256-3），在合成和体内数据集上达到了98.7%的中位分类精度。基于Cyclone V器件的FPGA原型以50 MHz运行，每个尖峰需要528个周期，对应0.01 ms的计算延迟，同时消耗828个ALMs和1023个寄存器，零DSP块。为验证ASIC可行性，基于FreePDK45工艺库实现了DBNN；Synopsys Design Compiler综合结果表明，在1.1 V供电、20 kHz工作频率下，估计硅面积为0.014 mm²，工作功耗为122 nW。这些结果表明，所提出的DBNN尖峰分类器在精度与实现成本之间提供了有利的权衡，支持低功耗植入式神经接口。总体而言，所提出的DBNN尖峰分类器以极低的硬件代价（0.014 mm²，20 kHz下122 nW）和无乘法器操作实现了高精度（98.7%），适用于低功耗植入式神经接口。本文首次提出了专为实时神经尖峰排序设计的DBNN，在输入数据大小与网络复杂度之间取得了出色的平衡。

</details>


### [162] [Unexpected Far-Near-Far Transition in Mobile Near Field Terahertz Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05630)
*Peng Zhang,Hanmei Yuan,Zhe Wang,Vitaly Petrov,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了太赫兹频率下倾斜接入点与地面用户通信时，由于链路距离和视角同时变化，可能导致近场-远场-近场（far-near-far）的多次传播模式转变现象，并推导了相应的解析条件。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有近场公式通常认为随着链路距离减小，传播模式只会从远场向近场转变一次。但本文发现，当接入点具有下倾角且地面用户移动时，这一直觉可能失效，实际可能出现多次远近场转变，需要对此进行理论分析。

Method: 针对倾斜均匀线性阵列（ULA）到点场景和均匀平面阵列（UPA）到点场景，推导了远近场转变发生的解析条件，并计算了地面上的对应转变点。

Result: 数值结果验证了分析的正确性，并进一步表明该行为强烈依赖于部署几何参数，且在较低频率下也可能出现。

Conclusion: 在倾斜接入点与地面用户通信的场景中，链路距离和视角的联合变化会导致意想不到的远-近-远（far-near-far）传播模式多次转变，这一现象受部署几何影响显著。

Original Abstract: At THz frequencies, the radiative near-field distance can be sufficiently large to matter in real deployments. Existing near-field formulas are often understood in a simple way: as the link distance decreases, the propagation regime is expected to change only once, i.e., from far field to near field. This paper shows that this intuition can fail for an elevated access point with downward tilt serving a ground user moving along the ground. Along such a path, the link distance and the viewing angle change together, so the near-field to far-field transition may take place more than once, creating an unexpected far-near-far transition. In this paper, we derive analytical conditions for when this transition occurs for tilted ULA-to-point and UPA-to-point scenarios and compute the corresponding transition point(s) on the ground. Numerical results validate the analysis and further show that this behavior depends strongly on the deployment geometry and can also arise at lower frequencies.

中文摘要翻译: 在太赫兹频率下，辐射近场距离可能足够大，从而在实际部署中产生重要影响。现有的近场公式通常以一种简单的方式理解：随着链路距离减小，传播模式预计只会发生一次变化，即从远场到近场。本文表明，对于具有下倾角的升高接入点服务于沿地面移动的地面用户这一场景，这种直觉可能失效。沿着这样的路径，链路距离和视角同时变化，因此近场到远场的转变可能发生多次，产生一种意想不到的远-近-远（far-near-far）转变。在本文中，我们针对倾斜均匀线性阵列（ULA）到点和均匀平面阵列（UPA）到点场景，推导了这种转变发生的解析条件，并计算了地面上相应的转变点。数值结果验证了分析的正确性，并进一步表明该行为强烈依赖于部署几何参数，且在较低频率下也可能出现。

</details>


### [163] [Sensor-Adaptive Infrared Spectral Reconstruction with Plug-and-Play Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05636)
*Alireza Siyavashi,Jon Schlipf,Sebastian Reiter,Inga Fischer,Christian Wenger,Christian Herglotz*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出伪逆引导扩散模型（ΠGDM），利用去噪扩散概率模型从窄带光电流中重建稀疏光谱，无需配对传感器测量数据，实现高精度、传感器自适应的计算光谱重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有高光谱传感器成本高、体积大，难以用于移动设备，亟需一种紧凑、低成本且精度高的计算光谱重建方法。

Method: 采用去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）在大规模公开光谱数据集上训练，学习真实光谱先验；推理时交替进行反向扩散去噪步骤和伪逆投影，通过传感器的标定响应矩阵确保与实测光电流的一致性。更换探测器阵列时仅需替换伪逆投影中的响应矩阵，无需重新训练扩散模型。

Result: 平均估计误差为1.502%，优于Tikhonov、高斯、压缩感知和MLP基线方法，并能通过蒙特卡洛采样提供校准的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该方法为资源受限平台上的光谱恢复提供了一种精确、紧凑的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral sensing enables material identification; however, state-of-the-art spectrometers are costly and bulky, which limits their use in mobile applications. We address this by proposing sparse spectrum reconstruction from narrowband photocurrents using a pseudoinverse-guided diffusion model (ΠGDM). With ΠGDM we use a denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM) to reconstruct the spectrum, which is trained on a large public spectral dataset to learn realistic spectral priors, eliminating the need for paired sensor measurements. At inference, ΠGDM alternates reverse-diffusion denoising steps with pseudoinverse projection to enforce consistency with measured photocurrents via the calibrated responsivity matrices of sensors. Consequently, our method is sensor-adaptive: when detector arrays change, we simply substitute the responsivity matrix in the pseudoinverse projection without retraining of the diffusion model. The resulting computational spectrometer achieves 1.502% average estimation error, outperforming Tikhonov, Gaussian, compressive-sensing, and multilayer perceptron (MLP) baselines, while providing calibrated uncertainty estimates via Monte Carlo sampling from different random initializations of ΠGDM. Summarizing, our approach offers an accurate, compact alternative for spectral recovery on resource-constrained platforms.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱传感能够实现材料识别；然而，现有的最先进光谱仪成本高昂且体积庞大，限制了其在移动设备中的应用。我们通过提出一种基于伪逆引导扩散模型（ΠGDM）的窄带光电流稀疏光谱重建方法来解决这一问题。ΠGDM使用去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）来重建光谱，该模型在大型公开光谱数据集上训练，学习真实光谱先验，从而无需配对传感器测量数据。在推理阶段，ΠGDM交替进行反向扩散去噪步骤和伪逆投影，通过传感器的标定响应矩阵确保与实测光电流的一致性。因此，我们的方法具有传感器自适应能力：当探测器阵列发生变化时，只需替换伪逆投影中的响应矩阵，无需重新训练扩散模型。所得到的计算光谱仪平均估计误差为1.502%，优于Tikhonov、高斯、压缩感知和多层感知机（MLP）基线方法，并可通过不同ΠGDM随机初始化的蒙特卡洛采样提供校准的不确定性估计。总之，我们的方法为资源受限平台上的光谱恢复提供了一种精确、紧凑的替代方案。

</details>


### [164] [Rydberg Atomic Quantum Radio: A Comprehensive Survey From Wireless Communication Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05931)
*Yiyue Xiang,Neng Ye,Qihao Peng,Junrui Zhao,Qu Luo,Kai Yang,Jianping An,Pei Xiao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文对基于里德堡原子量子（RAQ）的无线电接收机进行了全面综述，从原子物理与无线通信交叉视角，系统梳理了其量子机制、架构模型、性能增强技术、等效信道建模以及RAQ赋能的通信技术与部署场景，并展望了未来研究方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 下一代空天地海一体化网络对射频接收机提出了全频谱敏捷性、超高灵敏度和抗干扰韧性等前所未有的需求，传统电子接收机已逼近物理极限，因此需要探索基于里德堡原子量子的新型接收范式来突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 通过综述研究方法，首先介绍RAQ无线电的量子机制、典型架构和原子响应模型；然后系统梳理提升灵敏度、瞬时带宽和工作频率的先进技术及其内在权衡；接着分析等效信道建模框架以连接量子响应与通信理论；最后从无线通信视角综述RAQ赋能的认知通信、抗干扰通信、低频通信及MIMO通信等技术与部署场景。

Result: 系统总结了RAQ无线电在灵敏度、瞬时带宽和工作频率三个关键指标上的增强技术及其固有折中关系；建立了从量子响应到通信理论的等效信道建模框架；归纳了RAQ在认知通信、抗干扰通信、低频通信和MIMO通信等场景中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: RAQ无线电作为量子使能的新型接收范式，能有效缓解传统射频前端的瓶颈，但仍在灵敏度-带宽-频率权衡、信道建模、系统集成等方面面临开放挑战，未来需要进一步探索以推动其实用化。

Original Abstract: Next-generation space-air-ground-sea integrated networks (SAGSIN) impose unprecedented demands on advanced radio frequency (RF) receivers for full-spectrum agility, ultra-high sensitivity, and anti-jamming resilience, pushing conventional electronic receivers to their physical limits. To address these challenges, the Rydberg atomic quantum (RAQ) radio has emerged as a promising quantum-enabled receiver paradigm that directly maps electromagnetic fields onto atomic quantum states, offering an alternative to alleviate bottlenecks of conventional RF front ends. To provide a clear research roadmap, this survey presents a comprehensive review of RAQ radios by bridging atomic physics and wireless communications. Specifically, we first introduce the underlying quantum mechanisms, representative architectures, and atomic response models of RAQ radio. On this basis, state-of-the-art techniques for enhancing sensitivity, instantaneous bandwidth, and operating frequency are systematically reviewed, with particular emphasis on the inherent trade-offs among these key metrics. To connect quantum response with communication theory, we further analyze equivalent channel modeling frameworks for characterizing systematic performance limits. From the wireless communication perspective, some RAQ-enabled advanced technologies including cognitive, interference-resilient, low-frequency and multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) communications are reviewed, alongside emerging deployment scenarios such as satellite networks, integrated sensing and communications, and reconfigurable intelligent surface-assisted systems. Finally, we identify open challenges and provide potential future directions of RAQ radio to inspire the further exploration.

中文摘要翻译: 下一代空天地海一体化网络对先进的射频接收机提出了全频谱敏捷性、超高灵敏度以及抗干扰韧性的空前需求，推动传统电子接收机逼近其物理极限。为应对这些挑战，里德堡原子量子无线电作为一种有前景的量子使能接收范式应运而生，它直接将电磁场映射到原子量子态上，为缓解传统射频前端的瓶颈提供了替代方案。为提供清晰的研究路线图，本综述通过桥接原子物理与无线通信，对里德堡原子量子无线电进行了全面回顾。具体而言，我们首先介绍了RAQ无线电的底层量子机制、代表性架构以及原子响应模型。在此基础上，系统综述了提升灵敏度、瞬时带宽和工作频率的最新先进技术，并特别强调了这些关键指标之间的固有权衡。为连接量子响应与通信理论，我们进一步分析了用于表征系统性能极限的等效信道建模框架。从无线通信视角出发，综述了RAQ赋能的先进技术，包括认知通信、抗干扰通信、低频通信和多输入多输出通信，以及新兴的部署场景，如卫星网络、集成感知与通信以及可重构智能超表面辅助系统。最后，我们指出了RAQ无线电面临的开放挑战并提供了潜在的未来研究方向，以启发进一步的探索。

</details>


### [165] [Cell-Level Channel Shaping for Rydberg Atomic Quantum Receivers in Satellite Uplinks With Doppler-Enabled Superheterodyne Reception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05979)
*Qihao Peng,Qu Luo,Kezhi Wang,Cunhua Pan,Pei Xiao,Trung Q. Duong,Jiangzhou Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自超外差里德伯均匀阵列接收机，利用卫星运动自然产生的多普勒频移生成中频信号，并通过可编程本振设计优化信道容量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有卫星上行通信接收方案在射频-光转换建模不够精确，缺乏对空间变化本振电场的细致刻画，且未充分利用卫星运动引入的多普勒频移这一天然优势。

Method: 建立近场本振合成模型，刻画跨蒸气室的空间变化本振电场；基于蒸气室中心近似推导射频-光转换闭式解；将可编程本振建模为模拟域信道整形机制；构建最大化香农容量的本振优化问题并设计高效优化算法。

Result: 蒸气室转导可重塑有效信道、调整波束对准、适度降低用户间相关性；所提本振设计方案在可实现容量上显著优于基准方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自超外差里德伯均匀阵列架构为未来卫星通信系统提供了一种有前景的接收方案。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose a self-superheterodyne Rydberg uniform array receiver for satellite uplink communications, in which the Doppler shift naturally induced by satellite motion is exploited to generate the intermediate-frequency signal. We first develop a near-field local oscillator (LO) synthesis model and characterize the spatially varying LO electric field across the Rydberg vapor cells. Based on a vapor-cell-center approximation, a closed-form radio frequency (RF)-to-optical conversion is derived, establishing an explicit bridge between the incident satellite signal and the LO-induced cell-level response. The derived model reveals that the programmable LO serves as an analog-domain channel-shaping mechanism by controlling the cell-level transduction gain, phase response, and phase-matching behavior. Building upon this equivalent channel model, we formulate an LO design problem that maximizes the Shannon capacity of the effective channel, and develop an efficient optimization algorithm for the LO amplitudes and phases. Simulation results demonstrate that the vapor-cell transduction can reshape the effective channel, adjust the beam-pattern alignment, and moderately reduce the inter-user correlation under suitable LO configurations. Furthermore, the proposed LO design significantly improves the achievable capacity over benchmark schemes, offering a promising self-superheterodyne Rydberg architecture for future satellite communication systems.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出一种用于卫星上行链路通信的自超外差里德伯均匀阵列接收机，其中利用卫星运动自然产生的多普勒频移来生成中频信号。我们首先开发了近场本振合成模型，并刻画了跨里德伯蒸气室的空间变化本振电场。基于蒸气室中心近似，推导了射频-光转换的闭式解，建立了入射卫星信号与本振诱导的单元级响应之间的显式桥梁。所推导模型揭示出，可编程本振通过控制单元级转导增益、相位响应和相位匹配行为，充当一种模拟域信道整形机制。在此等效信道模型基础上，我们构建了最大化有效信道香农容量的本振设计问题，并提出了针对本振幅值和相位的高效优化算法。仿真结果表明，在合适的本振配置下，蒸气室转导能够重塑有效信道、调整波束对准并适度降低用户间相关性。此外，所提出的本振设计在可实现容量上显著优于基准方案，为未来卫星通信系统提供了一种有前景的自超外差里德伯架构。

</details>


### [166] [Mixture-of-Experts Deep Reinforcement Learning for Reliability-Constrained Energy-Efficient PDCCH Monitoring in Internet of Thing Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06084)
*Yue Xiu,Ning Wei,Zixian Song,Tianyu Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对5G物联网设备因持续监听PDCCH导致高能耗的问题，本文提出一种基于混合专家输入-输出隐马尔可夫模型（MoE-IOHMM）的预测性监听方案，在满足可靠性约束（误警率低于阈值）的前提下最小化长期平均能耗。仿真结果表明该方法相比始终监听和传统预测基线能有效降低能耗。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 5G物联网设备需持续监听PDCCH以检测下行控制信息，但无有效调度授权时仍要进行盲检，导致大量不必要的能量消耗。现有的预测性能量管理虽然可以通过跳过无授权时隙来降低接收机活动，但激进休眠可能错过调度授权，降低接收可靠性。因此需要在能耗与可靠性之间进行权衡。

Method: 将UE端PDCCH监听建模为可靠性约束下的长期能量最小化问题，提出基于混合专家输入-输出隐马尔可夫模型（MoE-IOHMM）的预测性监听方案。该模型包含多个IO-HMM专家以捕获异构的授权到达模式，并由一个门控网络自适应地组合各专家的预测结果，做出是否监听PDCCH的二元决策。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的MoE-IOHMM方案相比始终监听（always-on）模式和传统预测基线方法，有效降低了物联网设备侧的能耗，同时将误警率维持在预设的可靠性阈值以下。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MoE-IOHMM预测性监听方案能够有效解决5G物联网设备在PDCCH监听中的能耗-可靠性权衡问题，在满足可靠性约束的条件下显著降低长期平均能耗。

Original Abstract: The continuous monitoring of the physical downlink control channel (PDCCH) is a major source of energy consumption in fifth-generation (5G) Internet of thing device (IoT-D), since the UE has to blindly detect downlink control information even when no valid scheduling grant is present. Although predictive dynamic power management can reduce unnecessary receiver activity by skipping PDCCH monitoring in grant-free slots, aggressive sleeping may lead to missed grants and degrade reception reliability. To address this tradeoff, this paper formulates UE-side PDCCH monitoring as a reliability-constrained long-term energy minimization problem. Specifically, the IoT-D determines, before observing the actual scheduling outcome, whether to monitor the PDCCH or switch the receiver chain into a low-power state. The objective is to minimize the long-term average energy consumption, including receiver operating energy, component switching energy, and prediction-related computational energy, while ensuring that the false negative rate of scheduling-grant detection remains below a prescribed threshold. The resulting problem is non-convex due to the bursty and temporally correlated nature of grant arrivals, and the binary monitoring decisions coupled by a long-term reliability constraint. To solve this problem, we propose a mixture-of-experts input-output hidden Markov model (MoE-IOHMM)-based predictive monitoring scheme, where multiple IO-HMM experts capture heterogeneous grant-arrival patterns and a gating network adaptively combines their predictions. Simulation results show that the proposed scheme effectively reduces IoT-D-side energy consumption compared with always-on PDCCH monitoring and conventional predictive baselines, while maintaining the false negative rate below the prescribed reliability threshold.

中文摘要翻译: 在第五代（5G）物联网设备（IoT-D）中，物理下行控制信道（PDCCH）的持续监听是主要的能耗来源，因为用户设备即使在无有效调度授权时也需要盲检下行控制信息。尽管预测性动态功耗管理可以通过跳过无授权时隙的PDCCH监听来减少不必要的接收机活动，但激进的休眠可能导致错过授权并降低接收可靠性。为应对这一权衡，本文将UE侧的PDCCH监听建模为可靠性约束下的长期能量最小化问题。具体而言，物联网设备在实际调度结果产生之前，决定是否监听PDCCH或将接收链路切换至低功耗状态。目标是在确保调度授权检测的误警率低于预设阈值的前提下，最小化长期平均能量消耗，包括接收机运行能耗、组件切换能耗以及预测相关的计算能耗。由于授权到达具有突发性和时间相关性，且二元监听决策受长期可靠性约束耦合，该问题非凸。为解决该问题，本文提出一种基于混合专家输入-输出隐马尔可夫模型（MoE-IOHMM）的预测性监听方案，其中多个IO-HMM专家捕获异构的授权到达模式，门控网络自适应地组合其预测结果。仿真结果表明，与始终监听的PDCCH方案及传统预测基线相比，所提方案在保持误警率低于预设可靠性阈值的同时，有效降低了物联网设备侧的能耗。

</details>


### [167] [Bending Beam for THz Wireless Networks: Fundamental, Design Issue, and Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06294)
*Aoran Liu,Xin Tong Hu,Weidong Mei,Ya Fei Wu,Ke Liu,Boyu Ning,Zhi Chen,Rui Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文综述了弯曲波束在太赫兹无线通信与感知中的基本原理、波束赋形设计、硬件实现方法及关键应用场景，并通过硬件实验验证了其相较于传统波束聚焦的优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 弯曲波束具有非衍射和自修复特性，能够绕过障碍物解决太赫兹无线通信中的阻塞问题，但目前相关研究仍处于早期阶段，缺乏系统性综述。

Method: 首先对现有弯曲波束的波束赋形设计与硬件实现方法进行比较；其次讨论无线通信与感知中的潜在应用及挑战（如阻塞信道建模、波束训练、码本设计等）；最后通过太赫兹频段硬件演示验证弯曲波束的优势。

Result: 硬件演示表明弯曲波束在太赫兹频段上优于传统波束聚焦，能够有效绕过障碍物。

Conclusion: 弯曲波束为太赫兹无线通信与感知中的阻塞问题提供了新的解决思路，未来仍需在信道建模、波束训练和码本设计等方面进一步研究。

Original Abstract: Bending beams, characterized by their non diffracting and self-healing properties in the near field, offer a new approach to bypass blockage in terahertz (THz) wireless communication and sensing. However, the investigations of bend ing beams in the context of wireless communications still remain at an early stage. This article provides a state-of-the-art review of the fundamentals and key application scenarios of bending beams in THz wireless communications and sensing. We first present and compare the existing beamforming design and practical hardware implementation methods for bending beams. Next, we discuss potential applications of bending beams in wireless communica tions and sensing and identify their associated challenges, such as blocked channel modeling, bending beam training, codebook design, etc. Finally, a hardware demonstration of bending beam over THz frequency bands is presented, validating the advantages of bending beam over conventional beamfocusing.

中文摘要翻译: 弯曲波束在近场具有非衍射和自修复特性，为太赫兹无线通信与感知中的阻塞问题提供了新的解决途径。然而，弯曲波束在无线通信领域的研究仍处于早期阶段。本文综述了弯曲波束在太赫兹无线通信与感知中的基本原理及关键应用场景的最新进展。我们首先介绍并比较了现有的弯曲波束波束赋形设计与实际硬件实现方法。其次，讨论了弯曲波束在无线通信与感知中的潜在应用，并指出了相关的挑战，如阻塞信道建模、弯曲波束训练、码本设计等。最后，展示了太赫兹频段弯曲波束的硬件演示实验，验证了弯曲波束相比传统波束聚焦的优越性。

</details>


### [168] [Joint Probabilistic and Geometric Constellation Shaping for Complexity-Constrained Direct Detection Optical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06300)
*Rodrigo Fischer,Shrinivas Chimmalgi,Andrej Rode,Laurent Schmalen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出利用强化学习对直接检测光信号进行联合概率与几何星座整形，以在复杂度受限的联合均衡与解调中提升性能，并通过56 GBd、2.2 km C波段直接检测仿真验证了该方法的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在复杂度受限的直接检测光接收机中，传统的星座整形与均衡解调方案难以兼顾性能与计算资源约束，亟需一种高效的联合优化方法。

Method: 采用强化学习对概率星座整形和几何星座整形进行联合优化，以适配复杂度受限下的联合均衡与解调。

Result: 在模拟的56 GBd、2.2 km C波段直接检测系统中，所提方法在复杂度受限接收机上展现了有效性。

Conclusion: 强化学习联合优化概率与几何星座整形是提升复杂度受限直接检测系统性能的有效途径。

Original Abstract: We evaluate joint probabilistic and geometric constellation shaping via reinforcement learning for complexity-constrained joint equalization and demodulation of direct detection optical signals. We demonstrate the proposed technique in a simulated 56 GBd, 2.2 km C-band direct-detection system, demonstrating its effectiveness for complexity-constrained receivers.

中文摘要翻译: 我们评估了通过强化学习对直接检测光信号进行联合概率与几何星座整形的方法，以用于复杂度受限的联合均衡与解调。我们在一个模拟的56 GBd、2.2 km C波段直接检测系统中展示了所提技术，证明了其对复杂度受限接收机的有效性。

</details>


### [169] [A Hardware-Aware Open-Source Framework for Design Space Exploration of Mixed-Signal Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06456)
*Sayma Nowshin Chowdhury,Vineeta Nair,Taseen Forhad,Aishwarya Natarajan,Corey Hart,Sahil Shah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向混合信号脉冲神经网络的开源硬件感知仿真框架，支持神经元、突触和架构层面的跨层设计空间探索，并在多个神经形态基准上评估了分类精度、面积、功耗和量化敏感性等指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 边缘计算中能效神经形态硬件需要能够捕获混合信号SNN非理想特性的仿真工具，同时支持系统级设计探索，现有工具在跨层协同分析和物理参数优化方面存在不足。

Method: 构建开源硬件感知仿真框架，集成LIF、HH、AH等多种神经元模型及浮栅晶体管和ReRAM非易失性模拟突触，将器件级非线性特性直接嵌入PyTorch训练与推理流程，实现对物理突触参数的优化而非理想化抽象权重。

Result: 在N-MNIST、DVS Gesture和SHD三个基准上评估了每个模型-数据集配置的分类精度，同时报告了硅面积、功耗和量化敏感性等硬件导向指标。

Conclusion: 该框架能够实现跨层设计空间探索，帮助识别在精度、能效、面积和硬件保真度方面最优满足应用约束的神经元-突触配置。

Original Abstract: Energy-efficient neuromorphic computing at the edge requires simulation tools that can capture the non-ideal behavior of mixed-signal spiking neural network (SNN) hardware while supporting system-level design exploration. This work presents an open-source hardware-aware simulation framework for mixed-signal SNNs that enables comparative analysis across neuron, synapse and architecture choices. The framework supports multiple neuron models, including Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LIF), Hodgkin-Huxley (HH) and Axon-Hillock (AH), together with non-volatile analog synapses based on floating-gate transistors and ReRAM devices. By incorporating device-level nonlinearities directly into PyTorch-based training and inference, the tool enables optimization of physical synaptic parameters rather than idealized abstract weights. The framework is evaluated on standard neuromorphic benchmarks, including N-MNIST, DVS Gesture and Spiking Heidelberg Digits (SHD). For each model dataset configuration, it reports classification accuracy together with hardware-oriented metrics such as silicon area, power consumption and quantization sensitivity. These capabilities enable cross-layer design space exploration and help identify neuron-synapse configurations that best satisfy application-specific constraints on accuracy, energy efficiency, area and hardware fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 边缘节能神经形态计算需要能够捕获混合信号脉冲神经网络（SNN）硬件非理想行为的仿真工具，同时支持系统级设计探索。本文提出一个面向混合信号SNN的开源硬件感知仿真框架，支持跨神经元、突触和架构选择的比较分析。该框架支持多种神经元模型，包括泄漏积分点火（LIF）、霍奇金-赫胥黎（HH）和轴突-希尔（AH）模型，以及基于浮栅晶体管和ReRAM器件的非易失性模拟突触。通过将器件级非线性特性直接嵌入基于PyTorch的训练和推理流程，该工具能够优化物理突触参数而非理想化的抽象权重。该框架在标准神经形态基准（包括N-MNIST、DVS Gesture和Spiking Heidelberg Digits）上进行了评估。对于每个模型-数据集配置，它报告分类精度以及硅面积、功耗和量化敏感性等硬件导向指标。这些能力支持跨层设计空间探索，并帮助识别最能满足特定应用在精度、能效、面积和硬件保真度方面约束的神经元-突触配置。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Fast Alternating Radial Beamforming for Speed-of-Sound Imaging Based on Apparent Speckle Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06244)
*Dieter Schweizer,Can Deniz Bezek,Orcun Goksel*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于径向波束形成的超声声速成像方法，通过交替发射和快速配对交替波束形成实现运动鲁棒的位移跟踪，在标准硬件上达到20fps以上的成像帧率，是向实时声速成像迈出的重要一步。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有脉冲回波声速成像依赖笛卡尔网格波束形成，无法实现实时成像，且难以在资源有限、处理结构固定的常规超声系统中部署。

Method: 提出基于径向波束形成（结合虚拟源发射）的资源高效方法，实现在即时波束形成器中运行；引入交替发射序列与快速配对交替波束形成，用于基于典型线波束形成器的运动鲁棒位移跟踪。

Result: 数值仿真、组织模拟体模实验及乳腺病变在体数据表明：径向网格波束形成性能与笛卡尔网格方法相当，可在标准硬件上实现；所提序列可在常规B模式成像的同时达到20fps以上的声速数据采集帧率。

Conclusion: 基于散斑位移的径向方法配合同组波束线快速交替，是在资源适中的标准超声系统上实现实时声速成像的关键进展。

Original Abstract: Pulse-echo speed-of-sound (SoS) imaging based on minute misalignments between consecutively acquired ultrasound images traditionally relies on images beamformed on Cartesian grids. Existing SoS imaging developments do not allow for real-time imaging and typically do not prioritize feasibility in conventional ultrasound systems that have limited resources and rigid processing structures. In this work, we propose a resource-efficient approach based on radial beamforming with virtual source transmits for implementation within an on-the-fly beamformer. We also introduce alternating transmissions with fast pair-alternating beamforming for motion-robust displacement tracking with typical line-based beamformers. We tested these methods comparatively on numerical simulations, tissue-mimicking phantom experiments, and in vivo data from breast lesion examinations. We demonstrate that the proposed radial grid beamforming approach performs comparably to a Cartesian grid approach, while allowing implementation on standard hardware for beamforming. Our proposed sequences would allow for SoS data acquisition frame rates of more than 20 fps in parallel to conventional B-mode imaging. The proposed speckle-shift based radial approach with fast alternation between congruent beamforming lines is a major step towards real-time SoS imaging on standard ultrasound systems with moderate resources.

中文摘要翻译: 基于连续采集超声图像间微小错位的脉冲回波声速（SoS）成像传统上依赖于在笛卡尔网格上波束成形的图像。现有的声速成像方法无法实现实时成像，并且通常不优先考虑在资源有限、处理结构固定的常规超声系统中的可行性。在本工作中，我们提出了一种基于径向波束形成（结合虚拟源发射）的资源高效方法，用于在即时波束形成器中实现。我们还引入了交替发射序列与快速配对交替波束形成，用于在典型线波束形成器中进行运动鲁棒的位移跟踪。我们在数值仿真、组织模拟体模实验以及乳腺病变检查的在体数据上对上述方法进行了比较测试。我们证明，所提出的径向网格波束形成方法与笛卡尔网格方法性能相当，同时允许在标准硬件上实现波束形成。我们提出的序列可在常规B模式成像的同时实现超过20fps的声速数据采集帧率。所提出的基于散斑位移的径向方法配合同组波束形成线的快速交替，是在资源适中的标准超声系统上实现实时声速成像的一项关键进展。

</details>


### [171] [TMF-RSE: Tri-Modal Fusion with Regional Semantics and Evidential Uncertainty for Lung Severity Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06356)
*Fadi Abdeladhim Zidi,Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Abdellah Zakaria Sellam,Gaby Maroun,Fadi Dornaika,Cosimo Distante*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种三模态深度学习框架TMF-RSE，融合二维胸部影像、肺部分割掩膜和视觉-语言模型特征，结合证据回归实现肺部疾病严重程度量化与不确定性估计，在两个数据集上优于现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从胸部影像中准确量化肺部疾病严重程度对临床决策和资源分配至关重要，现有方法在多模态信息融合和不确定性估计方面仍存在不足。

Method: 提出TMF-RSE三模态深度学习框架：（1）融合二维胸部输入的外观特征、肺部分割掩膜的结构特征以及视觉-语言模型的语义特征；（2）采用互补融合机制，整合语义引导、结构先验和跨模态层次交互；（3）使用证据回归同时输出严重程度预测和不确定性估计。

Result: 在Per-COVID-19 CT数据集上验证集MAE为4.02，Pearson相关系数为0.9629；在RALO数据集上MAE为0.339，Pearson相关系数为0.973（地理范围评估），均优于近期基于Transformer的基线方法。

Conclusion: TMF-RSE通过三模态互补融合与证据回归，能有效提升肺部疾病严重程度量化的准确性和可靠性，同时提供不确定性估计。

Original Abstract: Accurate quantification of lung disease severity from chest imaging is critical for clinical decision-making and resource allocation. We propose a tri-modal deep learning framework, TMF-RSE (Tri-Modal Fusion with Regional Semantics and Evidential Uncertainty), that combines appearance features from two-dimensional chest inputs, structural features from lung segmentation masks, and semantic features from vision-language models (VLMs) for severity quantification. Our approach employs complementary fusion mechanisms that integrate semantic guidance, structural priors, and hierarchical interactions across modalities. The model employs evidential regression to provide both severity predictions and uncertainty estimates. Experiments on the Per-COVID-19 CT and RALO datasets show that TMF-RSE outperforms recent transformer-based baselines, achieving MAE of 4.02 and Pearson correlation of 0.9629 on Per-COVID-19 validation, and 0.339 MAE / 0.973 PC on RALO geographic extent.

中文摘要翻译: 从胸部影像中准确量化肺部疾病严重程度对于临床决策和资源分配至关重要。本文提出了一种三模态深度学习框架TMF-RSE（Tri-Modal Fusion with Regional Semantics and Evidential Uncertainty），该框架融合了二维胸部输入的外观特征、肺部分割掩膜的结构特征以及视觉-语言模型的语义特征，用于严重程度量化。我们的方法采用互补融合机制，整合了语义引导、结构先验和跨模态层次交互。该模型使用证据回归同时提供严重程度预测和不确定性估计。在Per-COVID-19 CT和RALO数据集上的实验表明，TMF-RSE优于近期基于Transformer的基线方法，在Per-COVID-19验证集上实现了4.02的MAE和0.9629的Pearson相关系数，在RALO地理范围评估上实现了0.339的MAE和0.973的Pearson相关系数。

</details>
